| BE-017  | P2       | Backend              | Drop the four orphaned `pilates_test_<hex>` databases left on the local server by interrupted integration runs (`0d8fe0d94fb4`, `3da52e3326cb`, `7384a2a4d627`, `73da0421e248`) and have `startDatabaseTestContext` drop its database when a run is interrupted (`SIGINT`/`SIGTERM` or an uncaught error before `stop`) | BE-002                          | done    | BE-014 leftover; disposable databases are dropped, never emptied (rows are never hard-deleted, ADR-0086 is about rows) **Compared with the code 2026-10-07** (twenty-fifth session). The row is right that interrupted runs leave `pilates_test_<hex>` databases behind and that they are dropped whole. How the context works today: `startDatabaseTestContext` (`test/database/database-test-context.ts`) is called from `beforeAll` by 50 integration specs directly and by `startEvaluationTestWorld` in `evaluation-test-support.ts`, and every caller stops it in `afterAll`. With `TEST_DATABASE_URL` (which must name a `pilates_test_*` admin database) it names a database `pilates_test_` plus 12 hex characters from `randomBytes(6)`, runs `create database` through a one-connection admin pool, migrates it with a synchronous `pnpm exec prisma migrate deploy` (`execFileSync`), and opens a pool; `stop` ends the pool and runs `drop database if exists "<name>" with (force)`. Without it, it starts a `postgres:17-alpine` Testcontainer with a `pilates_test_container` database and `stop` stops the container; nothing is dropped because the database lives inside the container, and Testcontainers' Ryuk reaper removes a container whose test process died (not exercised here: Docker is not running). What the row misses: (1) the only drop is in `stop`, so every path that skips `stop` leaks the database: Ctrl-C (the terminal sends SIGINT to the whole foreground process group, pnpm, Jest, and any running `prisma migrate deploy`), SIGTERM, SIGKILL, or an out-of-memory crash all end the Jest process on the default action without `afterAll`; (2) a failure inside the context after `create database` (a failed migration, or `pnpm` refusing the shell's Node 18) throws out of `beforeAll` before the caller holds a context, so `afterAll`'s `context?.stop()` has nothing to stop; three of the four orphans have no `_prisma_migrations` table at all, which fits a failure before Prisma ran better than a mid-suite interruption; `startEvaluationTestWorld` has the same gap when its fixtures fail after the context started; (3) a Jest timeout: `applyMigrations` blocks the event loop, so the 120 s `testTimeout` cannot fire during a migration, but a `beforeAll` that times out while `create database` is awaited makes Jest run `afterAll` with no context, and the database the late start creates is never dropped; an uncaught error inside a test only fails that test and `afterAll` still runs `stop`, so that case is already covered; (4) a signal handler cannot be registered from the context: `test:integration` runs in band (`--runInBand`, ESM through `--experimental-vm-modules`), so the tests run in the Jest process itself, but each test file sees a sandboxed copy of `process` (`jest-util`'s `createProcessObject` deep-copies it and reruns the EventEmitter constructor on the copy); a throwaway probe spec showed that neither `process.on('SIGTERM')` nor a listener on `import process from 'node:process'` runs when the Jest pid gets SIGTERM, and Jest died on the default action; `@jest/core` 29.7 registers no signal handler of its own, and a Jest `globalTeardown` does not run on a signal either; (5) CI cannot leave databases behind beyond a run: the `api` job's `postgres:17-alpine` service container, with its `pilates_test_ci` admin database and every `pilates_test_<hex>` the suite creates there, is discarded with the runner, so only a local server accumulates them. **Local server on 2026-10-07** (`psql -l`, creation times from each database's `PG_VERSION` file): the four orphans the row names, none with an active connection: `pilates_test_3da52e3326cb` (2026-09-22 11:16), `pilates_test_7384a2a4d627` (2026-09-22 14:11), and `pilates_test_73da0421e248` (2026-09-22 14:17), each empty (no `_prisma_migrations`, about 8.7 MB), and `pilates_test_0d8fe0d94fb4` (2026-09-23 11:06, 12 of today's 22 migrations, 33 application tables, 13 MB); two other `pilates_test_` databases the context did not create, since their names are not the generated 12-hex shape and nothing in the repository names them: `pilates_test_tz` (2026-09-22 01:37, 7 migrations) and `pilates_test_shadow` (2026-09-22 10:22, 8 migrations), listed here and not dropped without the user's say; and `pilates_test_fixtures`, the admin database for local integration runs, which is never dropped. The development databases `emma_pilates` and `pilates_dev_sim` are outside the item. **Done 2026-10-07.** Red `bbc30b65` (`test/database/interrupted-run.integration-spec.ts` starts a child Jest run of `interrupted-run.fixture.ts`, which starts a context, writes its database name to a scratch file, and waits, or throws before `stop`; the spec sends the child SIGINT, SIGTERM, or SIGKILL, or lets the throwing run end, then polls `pg_database` for that exact name for up to 15 s; 4/4 failed on that assertion, and the spec's `afterAll` dropped the four leaked databases by exact name) → green `a8d0ce59` (each disposable database gets a detached watchdog, `test/database/disposable-database-watchdog.ts`, spawned with `process.execPath` before `create database` and run by Node directly through type stripping; it holds the admin URL and the exact name, and when its stdin closes without `release`, because the test process ended without `stop`, it runs `drop database if exists "<name>" with (force)` for that one name, and does nothing for a name that is not `pilates_test_` plus 12 hex characters; `stop` drops the database itself as before, then writes `release` and closes the pipe) → refactor `365f7d81` (the spawn becomes `watchDisposableDatabase`, and comments say why it is a separate process); testing guide `1db77afa` (Test 8 «Real PostgreSQL integration tests»: Ctrl-C is safe, what the watcher drops and never drops, and how to check with `psql -l`). No migration, no production code, no OpenAPI or generated-client change. **Orphans dropped 2026-10-07** after confirming each still existed, had 0 rows in `pg_stat_activity`, and was neither `pilates_test_fixtures` nor a development database (`emma_pilates`, `pilates_dev_sim`), one `drop database "<name>"` statement each, by exact name: `pilates_test_0d8fe0d94fb4`, `pilates_test_3da52e3326cb`, `pilates_test_7384a2a4d627`, `pilates_test_73da0421e248`. Left on the server: `pilates_test_fixtures`, plus `pilates_test_shadow` and `pilates_test_tz`, which are not generated names and were dropped later on the user's say (leftover below). Verified 2026-10-07 from a clean shell (`env -u DATABASE_URL -u NODE_OPTIONS -u TEST_DATABASE_URL`): unit 116 suites / 574 tests; e2e 33 / 203; integration 66 / 293 with `TEST_DATABASE_URL=…/pilates_test_fixtures` (Docker not running; 138 s; the new spec takes about 12 s locally), and `psql` lists no new `pilates_test_` database afterwards and no watchdog process is left; two integration runs started through `pnpm --filter @pilates/backend test:integration` in their own process group and interrupted with SIGINT to the whole group, as Ctrl-C does (once mid-run with `pilates_test_5a350ac2d05f` alive, once right after `pilates_test_1fc9fca4a36e` appeared, with `prisma migrate deploy` running), each left no generated database within 0.2 s and no Jest, Prisma, or watchdog process; backend `pnpm lint` and `pnpm typecheck` exit 0. No simulator or browser surface applies (nothing user-facing changes; the change is test infrastructure only). Pull request https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/pull/8; CI run https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37697516658 green with the api chain only: changes 5 s, quality-api 1 m 48 s, contracts 1 m 3 s, api 6 m 52 s (unit 116/574, e2e 33/203, integration 66/293, `interrupted-run.integration-spec.ts` 20.5 s, so it ran against the service container rather than returning early); quality-web, web, quality-mobile, and mobile skipped by the paths filter. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: (1) the cleanup is a detached watchdog process per disposable database instead of a signal handler, because a handler registered from the context never runs (Jest's sandboxed `process`, proved by the probe) and a handler in a custom Jest environment would still miss SIGKILL, an out-of-memory crash, and a run that ends without `stop`; the watchdog covers every way the test process can end; (2) it is registered by spawning it with `detached: true` (its own process group, so Ctrl-C reaches Jest but not the watchdog) and `unref()` before `create database`, so no moment exists where the database lives unwatched, and it is removed by `stop`, which drops the database first and then writes `release` and closes its stdin; if the drop in `stop` throws, the watchdog stays armed and drops the database when the process ends; (3) it covers SIGINT, SIGTERM, SIGHUP, SIGKILL, crashes, a failed migration or failed fixtures after the context started, and a context left running after a Jest timeout, all at the moment the test process ends; a failed start in a still-running suite therefore keeps its empty database until the run ends rather than dropping it on the spot, which was not added because it would be a second mechanism for the same leak; (4) it lives in the context, not a Jest `globalTeardown`, because a global teardown does not run on a signal and does not know the generated names; (5) its scope is one exact name handed over by the process that generated it, refused unless it matches `pilates_test_` plus 12 hex characters, so it can never drop `pilates_test_fixtures`, the admin database, `pilates_test_shadow`, `pilates_test_tz`, or a development database, and nothing anywhere drops by pattern; (6) the watchdog is a `.ts` file run by Node 24's type stripping (no build step, still linted and typechecked; `.nvmrc` pins 24 for CI) and gets the parent's environment with `NODE_OPTIONS` cleared; (7) the Testcontainers path is unchanged: its database lives in a container that Testcontainers' Ryuk removes when the test process dies (not exercised; Docker not running); (8) the red test runs a real child Jest process over a fixture file that only the spec's `--testRegex` selects, so the normal integration and unit regexes never pick it up, and it skips without `TEST_DATABASE_URL`, like the existing context spec; (9) the orphans were confirmed through `pg_database` and `pg_stat_activity` immediately before dropping and dropped with a plain `drop database`, without `with (force)`, so a connection appearing in between would have made the drop fail rather than cut it off. Leftovers: none. `pilates_test_shadow` and `pilates_test_tz` (not generated names; last migrated 2026-09-22 to 7 and 8 of 22 migrations, holding 13 and 145 test rows, named nowhere in the repository or `apps/backend/.env`) were dropped on the user's say on 2026-10-07, after confirming each existed with no active connection, one plain `drop database "<name>"` each, by exact name; the local server now holds only `pilates_test_fixtures` among `pilates_test_` databases. |

| BE-008  | P1       | Backend              | Retention sweeps for expired idempotency records (≥ 48 h), expired/revoked Authentication Sessions, old throttle events, and read notifications      | BE-006, BE-007                  | done    | Policy red `97cdd37` → `47c803c` (`retentionCutoffs`); Prisma sweeps red `78df457` → `73e81d1` (`createPrismaRetentionSweeps`: parameterized `DELETE … WHERE id IN (SELECT … LIMIT n)` batches of 1 000, at most 50 per table per run, over existing indexes, so no migration); worker red `5f3b4c0` → `fa40f04` (at start and hourly, sanitized `retention_sweep_failed`), entrypoint `00531a8`; testing guide `588b1a3` (Test 16). Provisional windows (recorded as provisional until BE-007): idempotency records once past their own `expires_at` **and** at least 48 h old (commands set 24/48/72 h expiries, so the longer of the two wins); Authentication Sessions 90 days after revocation, idle expiry, or absolute expiry (keeps sign-in history for incident review); throttle events 30 days after `expires_at`; notifications 180 days after `read_at` (deliveries cascade; unread notifications are never swept; 180 days is far beyond any business event that could re-fire the same `business_event_key`). Never touched: audit records (append-only, BE-006), Accounts, Clients, Payments, Evaluations, receipts (their own ADR-0075 schedule). Verified 2026-09-23: unit 384/385 (the known pg-boss runtime env failure), worker spec 9/9, e2e 75/83 (the 8 known `app.e2e` role `test` failures), integration 36 suites/148 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL` (new real-PostgreSQL case: boundary rows on both sides of each window with batch size 1, an old audit record untouched, and a second run deleting nothing), touched files lint and typecheck clean; smoke on `pilates_dev_sim`: one stale row per table inserted, `node dist/src/worker-main.js` removed exactly those four (idempotency 36→35, sessions 88→87, throttle 7→6, notifications 24→23, audits 22→22) with no `*_failed` event. Gap: the windows are provisional; BE-007 may lengthen or shorten them (see its open questions). |

| BE-010  | P1       | Backend              | Give every date-bound integration spec an injected clock so none mixes the real clock with fixed 2026 date ranges; `booking-command` first, since every case but the reschedule one fails from 01/10/2026 | BE-009 | done    | Proof before the fix (2026-09-23): the whole integration suite on a scratch Jest config that fakes only `Date` (`fakeTimers` with `advanceTimers`, every timer in `doNotFake`) at 2026-10-15 10:00 Bolivia failed 18/201 in four specs; the same run of e2e passed 133/133. `booking-command`: red `21a6779` (`createFixture` now requires `now`; every case passes one file clock, pinned first to 2026-10-01 10:00 Bolivia: 12/13 fail with «No eligible Subscription») → green `7b325ae` (the clock moves to 2026-09-15 10:00 Bolivia, inside the 01/09–30/09 Subscriptions; the reschedule and idempotency cases keep their own clocks). `evaluation-test-support` (shared by the oversight, evaluation, back-office, catalog, and schedule-administration specs): `applyDevelopmentFixtures` planned Evaluation slots from the real clock while the world’s Subscriptions and `eligibilityMonth` are fixed to September, so `administration-oversight` failed 4/4 on `evaluations_slot_month_fkey`; red `8aebf24` (fixture clock injected at 2026-10-15) → green `dffd4bb` (`now: () => fixtureNow`). `booking-catalog-repository` «reads published Promotion metadata»: the image upload took `created_at` from the real clock against a fixed `expiresAt` of 01/10/2026 (`image_assets_valid_lifetime_check`); red `7e89cfe` (stamped 01/10) → green `dd72d81` (stamped 31/08, before the fixed display dates). `session-cancellation`: fixtures pinned to 21/09 with a real-clock Session filter; red `662e832` (clock 2026-10-15: «No eligible Subscription») → green `d3e4cc6`. Decision: this one is made fully relative (`today = boliviaDate(now)` with the real clock) instead of fixed, because Session cancellation stamps Bookings’ `cancelled_at` from the command clock while Prisma stamps their `created_at` from the real one, and `cancelled_at >= created_at` is enforced, so a fixed past clock cannot pass. Checked and left alone (already fixed or fully relative, or their real-clock values never meet a fixed range): `booking-catalog-concurrency`, `client-invitations`, `development-fixtures`, `upload-idempotency`, every other integration spec, and all e2e specs. No `pilates_test_*` databases left behind. Verified 2026-09-24: backend unit 101/102 suites, 476/477 tests (only the known `pg-boss-runtime.spec`); e2e 28/28 suites, 152/152 with `DATABASE_URL` on `pilates_test_fixtures`; integration 56/56 suites, 231/231 via `TEST_DATABASE_URL`, no leftover `pilates_test_*`; root `typecheck`, `check:api`, `check:api-compat` clean; admin web Jest 53 suites/347 tests in band. Surface walk on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded, `node dist/src/main.js` on 3100 and `worker-main.js`): Isabel (Mariana and Sofía already hold Plans from earlier walks) declared, put, finalized a 1170×2532 PNG receipt (`ready` in under a second), attached and submitted; `.data/uploads/private/transfer_receipt/<id>/{readable,thumbnail}` appeared and the pending original vanished; the receipt route answered 946×2048 JPEG without EXIF (default), 370×800 (`thumbnail`), `400` (`original`). Admin web at 1440 px (Pagos → Isabel Arce → Revisar pago: receipt in the panel, «Ver en grande» viewer; Esc closes) and 420 px (full-screen sheet, enlarged view), console only the pre-login `401` probes. Staff app in Expo Go on the iPhone 17 Pro Simulator with Maestro (Pagos pendientes → Isabel's card → Comprobante image → Ampliar full screen). Promotion API with node/curl: create with image (`draft`, `2400×1350`), replay with the same key returned the same row, a second key reused with other copy `409`, publish → `active`, admin list with `thumbnailUrl` serving 480×270 WebP, Lucía's `GET /promotions` listing three sources, the public 1440×810 WebP fetched without a credential with the immutable header, `promotion_image_required` for a draft without image, `promotion_order_incomplete`, `administration_access_denied` for Camila and Lucía, unpublish → public URL `404`, delete `204`, a 800×450 upload finished `failed image_dimensions_too_small`; restarting the worker swept the archived Promotions' 18 variants and every `public/`/`private/promotion` object. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: the receipt route keeps one URL and chooses the variant by query (default `readable`) so no client changes; profile photos are centre-cropped squares until an app screen supplies framing; WebP for Promotions, JPEG for receipts and profile photos; a Promotion image must be at least 1200×675 (AW18) and is centre-cropped to 16:9; delete archives (BKM-001) and releases the image; replacing or removing the image of a published Promotion unpublishes it (follow-up 39) and the old image's variants are released at once (ADR-0076); publishing copies the private variants to versioned public keys and re-publishing makes new ones; only the acting Administrator's own `promotion` uploads may be attached; create needs `Idempotency-Key`, the other commands address one identified transition; Promotion changes are audited except reordering; the local API serves the public images in place of the R2 origin (a `publicImageRoute` builder is the seam for EXT-002); no schema change was needed (BKM-001's `promotions` and UPL-001's `image_variants` already had every column). Reuse decisions: `runIdempotent` moved to `src/database` at its second user with an `onKeyReused` callback; the idempotency header shape and parser moved to `src/http` (Back Office, payments, and evaluations keep their own copies for now); `requireAdministrator` and `parsed`/`requireId` reused from `booking/administration`; payments' inline receipt attach was left in place (moving it onto `attachImageAsset` is a later refactor). Open questions for the user (data governance and product, not decided): how long archived Promotions, their copy, and their audit history are kept (BE-007); what a Promotion may show (purely informational per the glossary, but no content rule beyond the 80/280 characters is enforced); whether re-publishing should also require a display window that has not ended. Gaps: the review DTO's `receipt.mediaType` still names the upload's format (the caption reads «PNG» while the served variant is JPEG); WEB-006 may switch AW29's panel to `?variant=thumbnail`; the admin list has no filename for AW18's «filename + dimensions»; fixtures ship no Promotion (an image object cannot be seeded by the fixtures command); the public list is not paged and the reorder body caps at 200 ids; ended Promotions keep their public copies until unpublished or archived (the sweep does not watch the window); the Client app's receipt preview keeps its local file, as required; the iOS Simulator panel could not attach (`xcode-select` points elsewhere), so the Staff check ran through Expo Go and Maestro on the iPhone 17 Pro; no iPad run (the Staff review screen did not change). Walk rows kept: Isabel's pending Payment and receipt asset (money history), three archived Promotions and their deleted image assets, two failed `promotion` uploads awaiting the 24-hour cleanup. **Design follow-up 39 exported 2026-09-30** (`76a6f95`, status `5623d3b`): applied on a temporary Claude Design canvas (no TurboTenant design system), read back and copied byte for byte, rendered over a scratch copy of `design/` and checked against checklist 30 before the copy; the canvas was deleted. The design now draws what shipped: AW03 «Una sola vez» / «Cada semana» with «Día de la semana» and «Vigente desde» (generates 30 days ahead), «Solo esta sesión» / «Esta y las futuras» on a series Session, a one-off Session without the choice; AW07's required «Descripción»; AW17 cards with Borrador / Programada / Activa / Finalizada; AW18 «Guardar borrador» / «Publicar» (disabled until complete), «Guardar cambios» / «Dejar de publicar», and the image-change warning; purely informational sample Promotions on AW17, AW18, and 02; AW04 with the six catalogue plans. Decided under the blanket approval: no «Archivada» card (a deleted Promotion is archived and leaves the list, as built); «Vigente desde» may be today, as built. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 60 is in client 22 (centre-cropped circular preview) and AW18 (16:9 crop, WEBP, too-small message). |

| BKO-001 | P2       | Backend design       | Propose the Back Office schema for Supplies, Withdrawals, Salary Payments, Expenses, and Expense Categories                                         | Back Office glossary            | done    | Proposal `f667e78` ([BKO-001 proposal](./BKO-001-back-office-schema-proposal.md)) and migration review package `cc80aeb` ([review](./BKO-001-migration-review.md), [Prisma](./prisma/back-office.prisma), [generated SQL](./sql/back-office-review.sql), [protections](./sql/back-office-constraints.sql), [rejection cases](./sql/back-office-review-test.sql)), each committed alone; no migration written. Proposed `20260924000000_back_office`: creates the `back_office` schema and six tables (`supplies`, `withdrawals`, `supply_restocks`, `salary_payments`, `expense_categories`, `expenses`) with restrictive foreign keys to `booking.branches`, `booking.staff_members`, and `system.accounts`; no existing table changes. Verified 2026-09-23 on disposable databases (dropped): every shipped migration plus `prisma validate` on a merged copy; the generated SQL is `prisma migrate diff` output minus the ten known drift lines, and after applying it with the protections the diff shows only that drift; the review test passes all 31 expected failures plus a stock reconciliation check; two concurrent 6-of-10 Withdrawals left 4 units and one row. Decisions under the blanket approval (D1–D20): Administrator only for every command and read (recorder Account kept, so Assistants later need no migration); whole-unit stock kept by the database (`initial_quantity` fixed, `quantity_on_hand` changed only by Withdrawal/Restock insert triggers under the Supply row lock, never negative, always initial + restocks − withdrawals); **new Restock movement** (units in, no money) because nothing else can raise stock and AW09 forbids editing it; Withdrawals and Restocks immutable, no void; movement time is the recording instant, month by Bolivia month; low stock derived as at-or-below the threshold, badge only; Supply name and threshold editable, unique active name per Branch (`lower(btrim())` like the catalog), one-way archive instead of delete; Salary Payment editable (Staff Member of any Role, deactivated included, amount, `paid_on`); Expense Branch immutable, category/amount/`incurred_on` editable; amounts `> 0`; categories create/delete only, delete refused while referenced; archived Branches take no new records but are not blocked from archival; Read Committed (the row lock makes the stock change atomic, superseding the full release note's Serializable); Idempotency-Key on the four money/stock POSTs; `salary_payment.*`/`expense.*` recorded/updated audited (catalog rows added by BKO-002 with the writes), stock and categories not; no check compares a `now()` default with an application instant (the ADM-001a lesson). Open for the user (provisional answers built into the package): **Q1** may Salary Payments/Expenses be deleted or voided (provisional: no, edits audited); **Q2** future dates or locked months (provisional: up to today, no lock); **Q3** Salary Payments in a single-Branch AW16 report (provisional: all-Branches only, like transfer income); **Q4** retention and personal data (BE-007; provisional: keep indefinitely). Required with BKO-002: glossary entry for Restock and the at-or-below wording; design follow-ups for AW08 (Registrar reposición), AW09 (Archivar insumo), AW10 (insufficient stock), and AW16 per Q3. Known limitation: non-ASCII case folding in name uniqueness depends on the database locale, as for Plans and Class Types. |

Curl smoke on `pilates_dev_sim` as Paola: Supply create, Withdrawal 3 (7 left), exact replay (same id), reused key `409 idempotency_key_reused`, 50 units `409 insufficient_stock`, missing key `400`, Withdrawal 4, Restock 12 (15), AW31 month list with totals 7, category create, Expense Bs 450.50, category delete `409 expense_category_in_use`, Salary Payment Bs 3 500, future date `400 date_in_future`, report all Branches 395 050 and Centro 45 050 without payroll, anonymous `401`, Camila `403 back_office_access_denied`; one `expense.recorded` and one `salary_payment.recorded` audit row; API stopped. Decisions under the blanket approval: `Idempotency-Key` requests take a transaction-scoped advisory lock on the key so a concurrent same-key request always replays the first response instead of failing on stock (Read Committed, no Serializable, as D15 decided); replayed responses are the stored original, with dates rebuilt; the archive route is `POST …/{id}/archive` to match ADM-001's Branch and Class Type routes (the proposal sketched `/archival`); edits to an archived Supply answer `409 supply_archived`; the expense report takes the same `from`/`until` instants as `reports/income` and reads them as Bolivia civil dates; list date filters `from`/`to` are inclusive civil dates; Back Office rows are **not** added to the development fixtures, because the fixture world also feeds every integration spec and an empty Back Office is a valid start (records come from the API). Gaps: WEB-009 still has to combine `reports/income` and `reports/expenses` into AW16's net figure; design follow-ups 48–51 await the design export; the ADM-001a gap (other adapters leaving `created_at` to the database) is unchanged. |

Redefined by the user on 2026-10-07 while it was built, from four parallel jobs (quality, contracts, backend, frontend) to this shape | GitHub repository with Actions; GitHub Pro or a public repository for branch protection | blocked | Opened 2026-10-07 as the first gate to open after BE-014; the gate was already open (`git@github.com:DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates.git`, `gh` authenticated, no `.github/workflows` yet), so this was the next item: free, needs no provider, and all three backend suites run with no operator-set environment (`test:integration` only needs `TEST_DATABASE_URL` pointing at a `pilates_test_*` database on the service). **Compared with the code 2026-10-07** (twenty-third session, the first item built under the pull-request rule). Every script the workflow calls, timed from a clean shell (`env -u DATABASE_URL -u NODE_OPTIONS -u TEST_DATABASE_URL`, Node 24.13.0, pnpm 11.19.0, warm caches): `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile` 1 s with no prompt (the `allowBuilds` list decides what builds; the cold install on every runner also ran without a prompt), `prisma:generate` 2 s, root `lint` 16 s (only the backend and admin-web have a `lint` script: client-mobile, staff-mobile, and mobile-core have none, MOB-050, so the row's «lint» never covered mobile), root `typecheck` 14 s (six packages), admin-web `format:check` 2 s, backend `build` 5 s, admin-web `build` 8 s, api-contracts `build` 2 s, `check:api` 9 s, `check:api-compat` 2 s, backend unit 2 s (116 suites / 573 tests, 10 snapshots, so `--ci` is safe), e2e 6 s (33 / 203), integration 130 s (65 / 289 on `pilates_test_fixtures`), admin-web Jest 14 s (65 / 417), client-mobile 9 s (34 / 138), staff-mobile 13 s (33 / 115), mobile-core 5 s (50 / 217); per package for the split: backend `typecheck` 4 s, api-contracts `typecheck` 2 s, admin-web `lint` 4 s and `typecheck` 8 s, the three mobile `typecheck`s 2–3 s. Node 24 is what the tooling expects: no `engines` field anywhere, `@types/node ^24` in the backend and admin-web, the API and worker rely on Node 24's `require(esm)` since BE-004/BE-014, lockfile v9 for the `packageManager` pnpm 11.19.0, and the user's shell runs 24.13.0; `.nvmrc` says `24`. What the row missed, all found on the runners or by moving `apps/backend/.env` aside: (1) `prisma generate` and the OpenAPI generator behind `check:api` need `DATABASE_URL` to *exist* (`prisma.config.ts` calls `env('DATABASE_URL')` and throws `PrismaConfigEnvError`; the environment schema requires it) although neither connects, and the local `.env` hides it (without it: generate rc 1, `check:api` rc 1; unit, e2e, integration, typecheck, and build unaffected); (2) the in-band ESM integration run exceeds Node's ~2 GB default heap on the 4-vCPU runner after 47 of 65 suites (`FATAL ERROR: Reached heap limit`, [run 37667750246](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37667750246)), where the Mac's larger default hides it; (3) every Jest suite outside the backend passes and then never exits on Linux («Jest did not exit one second after the test run has completed»: the admin web for good, each mobile package after about five minutes), which on macOS only shows as «A worker process has failed to exit gracefully», so the frontend job hit its 15-minute timeout twice ([run 37667750246](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37667750246), [run 37669819289](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37669819289)); (4) `pg_isready` in the service health check runs as root inside the container and fills the service log with «role "root" does not exist»; (5) `ubuntu-latest` moves to Ubuntu 26 on 2026-10-19 (runner notice); (6) `client-mobile`'s `referrals-and-credit.spec.tsx` read the credit balance synchronously after Perfil opened and raced the balance request once on a runner ([run 37679121804](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37679121804), 1 of 138; green on the four runs before it; the re-run of that job was cancelled by the next push to the branch, which carried the fix and went green); (7) branch protection and rulesets both answer 403 «Upgrade to GitHub Pro or make this repository public to enable this feature» on this private repository (classic `PUT /branches/main/protection` and `POST /rulesets` both tried on 2026-10-07), so the protection half of the item cannot be applied by `gh`. **Built 2026-10-07** in [pull request #2](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/pull/2) (merged by the user at 19:53 UTC as `0d4bf792`): `c80b60b7` `.nvmrc`; `d9810c35` `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and the shared setup as the local composite action `.github/actions/setup/action.yml` (`pnpm/action-setup@v6` reading `packageManager`, `actions/setup-node@v7` reading `.nvmrc` with `cache: pnpm`, `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`, `prisma:generate`; `actions/checkout@v7`); fixes `bf543ff5` (the `test:e2e` and `test:integration` scripts keep the shell's `NODE_OPTIONS` next to `--experimental-vm-modules`, because a plain `NODE_OPTIONS=x cmd` in a script discards the shell's value, and the integration step sets `--max-old-space-size=4096`), `2a2c8362` (`pg_isready -U postgres`), `87801fd8` and `451fd7fb` (`--ci --forceExit` on every non-backend Jest step), `c5f85291` (`ubuntu-24.04`); the user's redefinitions `dc660829` and `087c533e` (a three-job api/web/mobile split asked for and reverted within the same minute), `84fb8bb7` (backend → api, frontend → web + mobile next to quality and contracts), `b0af6437` (quality → contracts → tests, so a cheap failure stops the run), `d0ebb143` (quality split per surface so each surface's tests wait only on its own checks); `6b0941ce` testing guide (committed on the item branch after the user had merged it, so cherry-picked into pull request #4 as `9068550b`; `f6d24b12` adds the filter paragraph). In [pull request #4](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/pull/4): `193f4ca8` the paths filter (`dorny/paths-filter@v3` in a `changes` job; a pull request runs a chain only when `apps/backend/**` or `packages/api-contracts/**` (api), `apps/admin-web/**` or `packages/api-contracts/**` (web), `apps/client-mobile/**`, `apps/staff-mobile/**`, `packages/mobile-core/**`, or `packages/api-contracts/**` (mobile), or a shared file (`package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `pnpm-workspace.yaml`, `.nvmrc`, `.github/**`) changed; a push to `main` always runs everything; the workflow gains `pull-requests: read` for the filter's API call), `37da7982` the referral spec waits for the balance. **Verified on GitHub 2026-10-07.** Green runs: [37672358904](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37672358904) (five parallel jobs, 7 min 45 s wall clock: quality 2:30, contracts 1:11, api 6:27, web 2:18, mobile 2:36), [37673437867](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37673437867) (chained, 10:38: quality 3:01, contracts 1:09, api 6:16, web 2:10, mobile 3:03), [37676619555](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37676619555) (seven jobs, 8:46: quality-api 1:52, quality-web 1:43, quality-mobile 1:11, contracts 1:10, api 5:32, web 2:28, mobile 3:11), the first push to `main` [37677887906](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37677887906) (8:17, all seven green), and the pull request #4 run [37680473112](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37680473112) (seven jobs behind the filter, 10:30: changes 0:05, quality-api 1:52, quality-web 1:44, quality-mobile 1:08, contracts 1:12, api 6:09, web 2:22, mobile 3:07). Runner minutes per clean run of all chains ≈ 17; the setup step costs 42 s per job; a cold runner is about three times slower than the warm Mac (unit 7 s vs 2 s, integration about 200 s vs 130 s). The checks bite, proven on scratch pull requests never merged (#3 and #5, closed, branches deleted): with the first layout, a Prettier violation in `main.ts` → quality red at `pnpm lint`, four jobs skipped, 1:24 ([37673562834](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37673562834)); `GET /api/v1/bookings/history` removed from `openapi.json` → contracts red at `check:api`, 4:32 ([37673801843](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37673801843)); a failing assertion in `back-office-schema.integration-spec.ts` → api red at `test:integration`, 9:30 ([37674439959](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37674439959)); an unformatted admin-web spec → quality red at `format:check` before web ran ([37675721949](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37675721949)). With the seven jobs: the three quality breaks together (Prettier in the backend, an unformatted admin-web spec, a type error in a mobile-core spec) → quality-api, quality-web, quality-mobile red at lint, `format:check`, and `typecheck`, the four test jobs skipped, 2:52 ([37676631021](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37676631021)); the removed operation plus a formatted failing admin-web assertion plus a failing mobile-core assertion → contracts, web, and mobile red, api skipped, the three quality jobs green, 4:26 ([37677085208](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37677085208)); the failing integration assertion alone → api red at `test:integration`, six jobs green, 8:58 ([37677806462](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37677806462)). The paths filter: a docs-only change → `changes` green, all seven skipped, 8 s ([37679205819](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37679205819)); a backend plus admin-web touch → api and web chains green, quality-mobile and mobile skipped, 7:43 ([37679314304](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37679314304)); a mobile-only touch → quality-mobile and mobile green, the other five skipped, 4:30 ([37681896341](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37681896341)); the filter reads the pull request's whole diff, not the last push, so the same mobile-only commit on top of the earlier backend and admin-web commits ran all three chains ([37680481474](https://github.com/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/actions/runs/37680481474)), which is the intended semantics: the merge result is what gets checked. Local cleanup: no server started, no disposable database created, the four orphaned `pilates_test_<hex>` databases still 4 (BE-017), `.env` restored, `dist/` rebuilt (untracked). **Blocked 2026-10-07 on the protection half**: the workflow is on `main` and runs, but `main` is unprotected (`protected: false`) because GitHub Free does not offer branch protection or rulesets on a private repository. To unblock, either upgrade the account to GitHub Pro or make the repository public, then apply exactly: required pull requests (no required reviewers), required status checks `quality-api`, `quality-web`, `quality-mobile`, `contracts`, `api`, `web`, `mobile` with `strict: true` (up to date with `main`), `enforce_admins: true`, `allow_force_pushes: false`, `allow_deletions: false`; the `PUT repos/DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates/branches/main/protection` body is in the twenty-third-session handoff. Until then the rule holds by convention only (the commit skill refuses `main`, the user merges). Readiness line 5 stays unticked (hosting waits for EXT-003). Decisions taken under the blanket approval: (1) the shared setup is a local composite action rather than steps copied into each job, so one file defines the toolchain and the workflow stays one file; (2) action versions pinned to major tags `actions/checkout@v7`, `actions/setup-node@v7`, `pnpm/action-setup@v6`, `dorny/paths-filter@v3` (the latest majors on 2026-10-07; `sha_pinning_required` is off on the repository); (3) the pnpm store cache is `actions/setup-node`'s `cache: pnpm` (keyed on `pnpm-lock.yaml`) rather than a hand-written `actions/cache` step, one less action and the same key; (4) `.nvmrc` says `24`, not `24.13.0`, so patch releases follow without a commit; (5) a workflow-wide placeholder `DATABASE_URL` on port 1 (`postgresql://nobody@127.0.0.1:1/pilates_ci_unreachable`, the BE-014 convention) satisfies `prisma generate` and the generator without any secret, and the integration suite overrides it per disposable database; (6) the heap flag reaches Jest through the package scripts keeping `${NODE_OPTIONS:-}` instead of the workflow calling Jest directly, so the scripts stay the only definition of how the suites run; 4096 MB because the run needed about 2.8 GB; (7) `--forceExit` on the four frontend suites instead of finding the open handles here (a leftover below), because the suites pass and the handle is not CI's concern; (8) the runner image is pinned to `ubuntu-24.04` because of the announced `ubuntu-latest` migration; (9) timeouts 15 minutes per job and 20 for api, so a hung step fails instead of running to the 6-hour default; (10) concurrency cancels superseded runs on every ref including `main`; (11) the `changes` job uses `dorny/paths-filter` (a third-party action, the standard one) rather than a `git diff` step, because it reads the pull request's file list through the API and needs no deep checkout; (12) `packages/api-contracts/**` triggers all three chains because `check:api` forces every contract change to touch the committed generated client, so a backend change that alters the contract always runs web and mobile too; (13) pushes to `main` ignore the filter so `main` always has a full green record; (14) the referral spec fix (a `waitFor` around the first balance read) was made here rather than queued, because a flaky required check would block every pull request; (15) the three-job split the user asked for mid-item was reverted with a revert commit, not a force push, so the branch history stayed linear; (16) the scratch proofs used force pushes and resets on their own throwaway branches, never on an item branch; (17) the row's description was rewritten to the user's redefinition instead of keeping the four-job text, because the user changed the definition, not the count. Leftovers, not taken: the open handles that keep the admin-web and mobile Jest processes alive on Linux (`--detectOpenHandles` from a Linux shell; likely the five-minute TanStack Query garbage-collection timer and MSW); the integration suite's in-band memory growth (~30 MB per suite file, 65 files), which the heap flag covers but does not cure; `apps/backend/package.json`'s `lint` glob still names non-existent `apps` and `libs` directories (BE-015 leftover); MOB-050 (mobile lint) still needs a row; the act() warnings the client-mobile and staff-mobile suites print on every run (24 000 log lines on the runner) are noise, not failures. |

## External accounts and provider gates

Work that needs an account, credential, domain, or real business data the
user must provide. Local stand-ins keep development moving; none of them may
reach production.

| Account or input | Needed for | Backlog items | What must be set up | Local stand-in today |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Expo / EAS account | Push tokens, EAS Build, Submit, and Updates | NOT-002, REL-001 | Organization, project IDs for both apps, push credentials, update channels | Expo Go and dev clients; in-app inbox only |
| Apple Developer Program | iOS builds, TestFlight, APNs, Universal Links | REL-001, NOT-002, LNK-001 | Team, bundle IDs `com.emmapilates.client` and `com.emmapilates.staff`, APNs key, associated domains | iOS Simulator |
| Google Play Console + Firebase (FCM) | Android builds, store release, push | REL-001, NOT-002, LNK-001 | App entries, signing key, FCM server credentials, `assetlinks.json` fingerprint | Android not verified yet |
| SendPulse | Real authentication email | EXT-001, EML-001 | Sender domain with SPF, DKIM, DMARC; API credentials; bounce/complaint webhook | `LocalEmailCapture` in the worker |
| Cloudflare R2 | Receipt, profile, and Promotion image storage | EXT-002, UPL-002 | Private and public buckets, scoped keys, public custom domain for Promotions | `LocalObjectStorage` under `.data/uploads` |
| Northflank + Aiven PostgreSQL | Production API, worker, migrations, database | EXT-003 | Services, secrets store, backups, staged migration job | Local API, worker, and PostgreSQL |
| Render + Neon | Shared development API and database for previews | EXT-003 | Services and development secrets | `pilates_dev_sim` locally |
| Vercel | Admin-web previews and production | EXT-003, WEB-010 | Project, protected previews, `/api/v1` proxy | Vite dev server |
| GitHub Actions | CI and deployments | EXT-004 (CI, done 2026-10-07), EXT-003 (deployments), BE-003 | Workflows, disposable PostgreSQL, deployment-only credentials; branch protection on `main` needs GitHub Pro or a public repository (403 on a private repository under GitHub Free) | CI runs on every pull request and push to `main`; `main` protected by convention only |
| Better Stack | Error reporting, uptime, and heartbeat alerts | BE-005 gap, EXT-003 | Sources, monitors, email alerts | JSON logs on stdout |
| Domain and DNS | API host, admin host, mobile-link domain, email sending domain | LNK-001, EXT-001, EXT-003 | Registered domain and records | `localhost` and custom URL schemes |
| Studio business data | Transfer instructions and QR shown to Clients | Production config | Real `TRANSFER_BENEFICIARY_NAME`, `TRANSFER_BANK_NAME`, `TRANSFER_ACCOUNT_NUMBER`, QR image | Placeholder values in `.env.example` |
| Production secrets | Link encryption and email digests | EXT-003 | Fresh `AUTHENTICATION_EMAIL_DIGEST_KEY`, `AUTHENTICATION_LINK_ENCRYPTION_KEY(_ID)` with a rotation plan, `RELEASE_ID` per deploy | Development-only keys |

### Order to open the gates (agreed 2026-10-07)

Every code item without an account gate is done since BE-014, so the order
below is the project's priority order; each gate names the item it unblocks.

1. GitHub repository with Actions → EXT-004 (CI on the three backend suites).
   Already open: the repository is `DaleneyRuben/emma-pilates`; CI is built and
   green since 2026-10-07. What remains of EXT-004, protecting `main`, needs
   GitHub Pro or a public repository (branch protection and rulesets answer
   403 on a private repository under GitHub Free).
2. Domain and DNS → the API, admin, mobile-link, and email-sending hostnames
   that LNK-001, EXT-001, and EXT-003 verify against.
3. SendPulse → EXT-001 (real authentication email; readiness line 3).
4. Expo/EAS and Apple Developer → REL-001 account parts (bundle IDs, EAS Build
   and Submit, TestFlight smoke), then NOT-002 push on iOS, then LNK-001
   Universal Links.
5. Northflank + Aiven, Render + Neon, Better Stack → EXT-003 (production and
   shared development hosting, staged migrations, backups, monitoring).
6. Cloudflare R2 → EXT-002.
7. Google Play Console + Firebase → the Android halves of REL-001, NOT-002, and
   LNK-001 (Android has never been verified; expect fixes).
8. Vercel → WEB-010.
9. Studio business data and production secrets → the production configuration
   at go-live.

Meanwhile, without any gate: BE-016, then BE-017 (BE-015 is done since
2026-10-07); MOB-050 (mobile lint, so the quality-mobile job can lint) still
needs a row. FUT-001 stays deferred.

**Handoff (2026-10-08, thirtieth session: MOB-055, MOB-057).** Two pull requests are open for the user to merge (merge commits, never squash), none stacked, both branched from `main` with pull requests 12–15 merged: **16** `chore/mob-055-maestro-suite-hygiene` (MOB-055 done: the booking flow defaults to Lucía, the photo flow states its worker and library-photo needs, the release smoke script lives in `@pilates/tooling` as the `pilates-mobile-release-smoke` bin with its own ESLint config and a lint step in quality-mobile, the four Staff flows park Expo Go's gear by element so they pass on the iPad as committed, the client suite order is pinned with `close-account` last, Test 19 rewritten; CI run 37817960004 green with the full chain at the first attempt and run 37819439157 green again after the mark-done commit) and **17** `chore/ci-drop-forceexit` (MOB-057, a draft: the flag was dropped, CI run 37819179289 showed the mobile suites linger five to six minutes each on the Linux runner and the job timed out, so the workflow change is reverted on the branch and the row is redefined as finding that Linux-only handle; merging it lands the row and a Test 8 note only). Merge 16 first; 17 touches `docs/implementation/BACKLOG.md` one row below MOB-055 and Test 8 a few lines from 16's own Test 8 edit, so expect at most a trivial conflict in the second merge (keep both sides). Verified from a clean shell on 16 (lint 0 errors and 0 warnings in tooling and the three mobile packages, `format:check`, typecheck, Jest `--ci` twice without `--forceExit`: mobile-core 53/233, client-mobile 35/142, staff-mobile 34/118, 0 `console.error`; both `release:smoke` green through the bin) and on 17 (the same three suites twice in its worktree). Surface for 16: every committed flow of both apps as committed on the iPhone 16 Pro and the iPad Pro 11, API on a rebuilt `pilates_dev_sim` with fresh fixtures, the worker, both Metros (8083 and 8082), the Promotions and Lucía's two notifications seeded through the API, one PNG in each library: Client 6 of 6 and Staff 4 of 4 on both devices (the Staff suite 2 of 4 on the iPad before the gear change, the red). The API, the worker, both Metros, and both simulators are stopped; the throwaway worktrees are removed; nothing temporary is left in the tree (the only local-only artefact is pnpm's bin shim copied by hand into both apps' `node_modules/.bin`, which a fresh `pnpm install` recreates). **Next to pull: MOB-057** as redefined (`ready`, P3, under «Mobile-first queue»: find the Linux-only open handle, then drop the flag), from `main` once 16 and 17 are merged; after it the queue's next open rows. Follow-up 89 still waits for its export; the next free follow-up number is **90**. EXT-004 stays `blocked` on protecting `main`; NOT-002, LNK-001, WEB-010, and REL-001's account parts stay blocked externally. **Decisions taken this session** (under the blanket approval, detailed in the rows): Lucía as the booking default; the photo flow leaves the library seeding to the operator; the smoke script moved into tooling as a bin with its own narrow Node ESLint config and a quality-mobile lint step, no Prettier for tooling; the gear parked in the flows by an element swipe guarded by visibility instead of a manual recipe; the client suite order pinned; Test 19 rewritten with the `-e` override claim retracted; the Home flow's notifications seeded by hand through the documented endpoints, no fixture added; the local bin shim copied by hand after pnpm refused to relink; MOB-057's workflow change reverted and the row redefined rather than left red or marked blocked; the admin-web step untouched. Lessons: Expo Go's gear is the `gearshape.fill` element and a `swipe` from it works on both devices; a flow's `env:` beats `-e`; Maestro's folder order is not the file order without `flowsOrder`; the mobile suites exit at once on macOS but linger on Linux; pnpm 11 reports «Already up to date» and relinks nothing after a `bin` change.

**Handoff (2026-10-08, twenty-ninth session: MOB-053, MOB-054, MOB-056; rows MOB-054–056).** Four pull requests are open for the user to merge (merge commits, never squash), none stacked, each branched from `main` with pull request 11 merged and the others still open: **12** `feature/mob-053-error-boundary` (MOB-053 done: `RenderErrorBoundary` and `RecoveryScreen` in mobile-core, the root of `MobileApplication` and every screen of the eight stacks wrapped through React Navigation's `screenLayout`, follow-up 89 written for the recovery screen, Tests 1 and 8 updated; CI run 37735033808 green), **13** `chore/backlog-mob-054-056` (backlog-only: the three rows, one commit each, status `ready`), **14** `chore/mob-054-mobile-jest-act-warnings` (MOB-054 done: both app setups fail a spec on any React act warning, every asynchronous RNTL call awaited, TanStack notifications synchronous, the act environment declared, Test 8 updated; the open handle turned out not to exist; CI run 37739820505 green), and **15** `fix/mob-056-link-during-restore` (MOB-056 done: a link that arrives while a kept registration is loading keeps its screen, two specs, Test 5 updated; CI run 37780139344 green; this handoff). Because the rows pull request was still open when MOB-054 and MOB-056 were built, each of those branches carries its own copy of its row with the evidence, so the second of the two pull requests to merge conflicts with 13 on that one line: merge 13 first, then 14 and 15, keeping the item branch's line in each conflict (the row with the evidence). ## Production readiness checklist

Nothing reaches real users until each line is done:

- [x] Sessions materialize without fixtures (BKM-003) and empty Sessions cancel themselves (BOOK-004).
- [x] First Administrator bootstrap command and runbook (IDN-006); a production run still needs real email (EXT-001).
- [ ] Real email templates and SendPulse delivery (EML-001, EXT-001); R2 storage (EXT-002; the variants exist since UPL-002).
- [ ] Push delivery and store builds (NOT-002, REL-001) with Universal/App Links (LNK-001).
- [ ] Hosting, CI, secrets, staged migrations, and monitoring (EXT-003, BE-005 gaps, BE-003).
- [ ] Data governance: Account closure, personal-data and Evaluation retention, audit retention, backups, restore drills, incident runbooks (BE-006, BE-007, BE-008).
- [ ] Staff management and admin web for studio operation (IDN-007, ADM-001, BKO-001/002, WEB-001–WEB-010).
- [ ] Real transfer instructions and production secrets configured (see the gate table above).
- [x] The backend suites are green with no operator-set environment (BE-014): the e2e suite boots without a database and the unit suite needs no `--experimental-vm-modules`.

## Admin-web queue

Admin web is the lowest-priority application surface. It is still necessary for
full studio operation before production; local fixtures temporarily provide the
catalog and schedule data needed to build mobile first.

the resolver takes the expected surface, rejects a mobile credential presented by the browser and a browser credential presented as a bearer, and extends browser idle by 30 min capped at absolute); Administrator-only Login red `d6db00b` → `711e18a`, `111af93`, `d6e34cc` (`login` gets `surface`; `findAccountByEmail` reads the Staff Role in the same query and a non-Administrator with the right password gets `AuthenticationFailedError` after the same Argon2id work and is recorded as a failed attempt, ADR-0029); browser recovery red `a5922d8` → `bf4575f` (`completePasswordRecovery(…, 'admin_web')` passes `sessionEligibility: 'administrator'`; the gateway replaces the password, revokes every session, and opens the ADMIN_WEB replacement only for an Administrator, all in one transaction with the existing `account.password_recovered` audit); trusted origins red `8e1d3e9` → `11019d7` (`ADMIN_WEB_TRUSTED_ORIGINS`: comma-separated exact http(s) origins, no wildcard, path, or bare host; default `http://localhost:5173` outside production; production requires explicit https origins); transport red `b416555` → `1467801` (`__Host-emma-admin-session` HttpOnly Secure SameSite=Lax Path=/ Max-Age 12 h with no Domain, never in a body; `__Host-emma-admin-client` random 43-char identifier as the ADR-0029 browser throttling signal, 180 days; a request middleware refuses `403 untrusted_origin` for every unsafe method authenticated by the cookie and for every `/api/v1/admin-web/*` unsafe request (Login CSRF), strips any caller-sent `AdminSession` scheme, and hands the cookie to the ordinary principal resolution so every existing Administrator endpoint and `PATCH /accounts/current/password` work from the browser; `POST/GET/DELETE /admin-web/authentication-sessions[/current]` and `POST /admin-web/password-recoveries` returning `{status, signedIn}`; `toLoginProblem` shared with the mobile controller), wiring `eea9605` (`adminWebProviders`, shared `loginDependencies`/`passwordRecoveryDependencies`, middleware on every route; the oversized module shrank 462 → 461 lines), contracts `3a78a28` (additive; `adminWebSession` cookie security scheme), compatibility exemption `f674116`. Admin web: session expiry red `6668cd9` → `93adc82` (`createSessionExpiry`: the first `authentication_required` from any query or mutation returns to Login once; the startup probe, `authentication_failed`, and anything after logout are ignored); guard red `be54b46` → `04e7a57`, env `5f87ce2` (Node `AbortController`/`AbortSignal` in the Jest environment so TanStack's signals reach Node fetch; `createAdminApp` owns QueryClient + router; pathless `authenticated` layout whose `beforeLoad` reads `GET /admin-web/authentication-sessions/current` through `ensureQueryData` and redirects to `/iniciar-sesion?redirect=…`; Login redirects a signed-in Administrator to Horario; a probe that cannot reach the backend shows Reintentar instead of Login; an ended session clears the cache and returns to Login with 'Tu sesión terminó'); AW01 red `97645f4` → `1c3919b` (React Hook Form + Zod through the form primitives, codes `authentication_failed`/`login_throttled`, countdown only from `Retry-After`, same-app redirect only); AW30 red `d7e22e5` → `af2b218`; AW32 red `ff786bc` → `8408c76` (`/crear-contrasena?token=` inspects first; the inspected purpose picks Activar cuenta or Restablecer contraseña; expired/used, invalid, and used-while-open states; activation returns to Login with a notice, recovery signs in to Horario or returns to Login when no browser session opened); AW33 red `b2cb2f5` → `2815327` (initials, name, and email from `GET /staff-members/current`; popover 240 px / bottom sheet; 408 px side panel / full-screen sheet; `current_password_incorrect` on its field; logout confirmation, cache cleared); class-merge fix red `9428ca8` → `ac0585f` (tailwind-merge did not know the Emma font sizes and silently dropped `text-blanco` from every primary button and `text-titulo-panel`/`text-etiqueta-campo` wherever a color followed, a WEB-001 bug found in the browser); design follow-up 52 `cc46e99`; audit catalog `d692ef6`; testing guide `86aee4e` (Test 24). Verified 2026-09-23: backend unit 97/98 suites, 441/442 tests (only the known `pg-boss-runtime.spec`), e2e 22/22 suites, 123/123 tests with `DATABASE_URL` on `pilates_test_fixtures` (the 8 `app.e2e` role failures of earlier rows no longer appear), integration 47/47 suites, 193/193 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL` with no leftover databases, touched backend files lint clean and `tsc` clean, `pnpm check:api` clean, `pnpm check:api-compat` green; admin web Jest 14 suites/93 tests, `eslint .`, `tsc -b`, Prettier, and `vite build` (560 kB JS) clean. Curl smoke on `pilates_dev_sim`: Paola login 201 with both `__Host-` cookies, current and `GET /staff-members/current` through the cookie, Camila `401 authentication_failed`, cross-origin DELETE and PATCH `403 untrusted_origin`, logout 204 with `Max-Age=0`, old cookie 401; the stored row is `admin_web` with 30 min idle and 12 h absolute. Browser journey in Playwright-driven Chromium 149 (headless and headed) through `pnpm dev:admin` at 1440 px and 420 px: `/catalogo/planes?page=2` redirected to Login, Camila rejected neutrally with her email kept, Paola returned to Planes; both cookies host-only (`domain: localhost`), HttpOnly, Secure, Lax, session 12 h, `document.cookie` empty; menu, 408 px panel with a wrong-current-password field error, cross-origin logout refused while the session stayed valid, confirmed logout cleared the cookie and the old credential answered 401; recovery sent state, invalid-link state, the 7th failed Login showed the 0:30 countdown with a disabled button; at 420 px a bottom-sheet menu and a full-screen password panel; primary button text computed white and the panel title 22 px Lora after the class-merge fix. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: one browser Login lane shared with mobile (same email/client/network throttle rules), with the client signal from the `__Host-emma-admin-client` cookie; `__Host-` cookie names so the browser enforces host-only and Secure; Max-Age equals the 12 h absolute lifetime (the server still enforces idle); only unsafe methods are origin-checked (GET is safe and SameSite=Lax), a missing Origin is refused, and bearer requests from the apps are never origin-checked; the cookie reaches other contexts' endpoints as an internal `AdminSession` credential rather than a new guard on 168 handlers; `GET …/current` answers 401 once the Staff Member is no longer an Administrator (demotion ends browser access); logout always clears the cookie and answers 204 even if the session had already ended; a non-Administrator who completes a recovery link in the browser gets the password replaced and every session revoked but no browser session (`signedIn: false`), because the link proves mailbox control and recovery is Account-wide (ADR-0030); link inspection keeps one expired state for expired and already used links (the backend does not distinguish them); one AW32 route `/crear-contrasena?token=` for both link purposes, chosen by the inspected purpose (the email URL shape is EML-001's); an unknown address shows Página no encontrada without the shell or the guard; activation, recovery request, inspection, and password change reuse their existing operations, so only four new admin-web-only operations exist; the admin menu labels the Role «Administración» like the Staff app's S23 instead of AW33's «Administradora» (follow-up 52); client passwords need at least 8 characters, the mobile rule; untrusted-origin refusals and browser Login/logout are not audited (catalog). Gaps: the real Vercel proxy (forwarded client address, `trust proxy`, preview-origin registration) stays in WEB-010/EXT-003; emails still carry the placeholder `pilates://` link, so an Administrator's link needs EML-001 to point at `/crear-contrasena`; valid activation and recovery links cannot be read from the local email capture, so a successful activation or reset in the browser is covered by automated tests only; an expired invitation link cannot yet be told apart from one removed by the 30-day cleanup (follow-up 35 asks for both); Safari may refuse `Secure` cookies on `http://localhost`; the bundle passed Vite's 500 kB hint, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010; no Log out all other devices action yet (ADR-0030) and background polling does not exist yet to exempt from idle extension; Playwright stays a scratch script until WEB-010 adds the committed suite. AW02's narrow drawer still says «Administradora» (52 did not target it): carried by follow-up 70. |

Verified 2026-09-23: backend unit 97/98 suites, 441/442 tests (only the known `pg-boss-runtime.spec`); e2e 23/23 suites, 127/127 tests with `DATABASE_URL` on `pilates_test_fixtures`; integration 47/48 suites, 197/198 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL` (the one failure is `booking-command` «reschedules a Booking to a same-day Session», a time-of-day flake: run at 21:05 Bolivia its target Session at now + 3 h falls on the next Bolivia day; untouched here), no leftover `pilates_test_*` databases; backend typecheck clean; `pnpm check:api` clean, `pnpm check:api-compat` 2/2; admin web Jest 24 suites/144 tests, `eslint .`, `tsc -b`, Prettier, and `vite build` (675 kB JS, 29 kB CSS) clean. Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium (playwright-core 1.61.0 from the scratchpad) against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (locale es-BO, browser zone Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (browser zone Asia/Tokyo): the grid showed Centro's Sessions at the same Bolivia times in both zones; empty-submit validation on every field; a one-off Mat Pilates on Tuesday 12:00 («Sesión creada.»); a Friday 12:00 weekly schedule from next Monday («Serie semanal creada: 4 sesiones en los próximos 30 días.»); «Esta y las futuras» to 13:00 from 02/10 («4 sesiones nuevas; 4 sin reservas se reemplazaron», the 0-kept wording then fixed in `d8ee6c4`); «Terminar la serie» from 03/10; switching to Equipetrol redrew the grid; two Client Bookings made through the API on Tuesday's 09:00 Mat Pilates, then at 420 px capacity 1 was refused on the field («Debe ser al menos 2.»), the cancel dialog named the refunds, and the page said «Sesión cancelada: 2 reservas reembolsadas y avisos enviados.»; Sucursales create/edit/archive of a test Branch, Centro refused with `branch_in_use`, the Archivadas filter; a Class Type named «Mat Pilates» refused on its field (`class_type_name_taken`); a 6-month 90-class Plan at `Bs 5.400,00`, then hidden and found under Ocultos; bottom-sheet selector, full-screen panels, card tables, and day tabs at 420 px; no console errors besides the pre-login `401` probe and the two expected `409`s. The walk's test Branch, Class Types, and Plans were deleted from `pilates_dev_sim` afterwards; its schedule changes stay. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: page-and-offset catalog reads with a status or visibility filter instead of client-side paging over arrays (ADR-0061/0062); the Horario grid reads Sessions, not schedules, and shows only scheduled ones (a cancelled occurrence disappears; the week cap is the backend's 100, flagged on screen when reached); the week lives in the URL (`?week=`, Monday), the narrow day tab and open panels in local state; Trainers come from `listStaffMembers?role=trainer&branchId=` without deactivated ones; «Cancelar sesión» appears only when the Session has Bookings, because the backend refuses an empty one and the 2-hour check cancels it (follow-up 53 §1); a Session's Class Type is read-only on edit (ADM-001 C4); «Esta y las futuras» keeps the schedule's Class Type, defaults Vigente desde to the occurrence date, and never offers a date before the backend's rule (today and after the schedule's start); ending a schedule lives under «Esta y las futuras» as «Terminar la serie» (follow-up 53 §3); mutations invalidate exactly the selected Branch's week keys (a predicate on `branchId`) and its schedules, catalog changes every list of that catalog (asserted by `schedule-invalidation.spec.tsx`); Plans get «Orden en la app» and «Ocultar/Mostrar en la app», Branches and Class Types an Estado filter and an archive action (follow-up 53 §5–6); the field label stays «Entrenador» as AW03 draws it. Reuse decisions: admin web writes its own `formatBolivianos` and Bolivia-time helpers because `@pilates/mobile-core` is a React Native package and its formatter drops the decimals ADR-0082 and the design system show; a shared `CatalogPage` layout was extracted when the second catalog page appeared and Sucursales moved onto it; `ChoiceChips` is shared by the frequency and scope choices; the API-002 query was extended rather than duplicated. Gaps: native date and time pickers follow the browser's language (an English Chrome shows `09/29/2026` and `09:00 AM`), so dd/mm/aaaa and 24-hour pickers need custom controls or a Spanish browser; cancelled Sessions are not drawn (AW02 shows a «Cancelada» legend); the bundle is 675 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010; the `booking-command` reschedule spec needs a fixed clock (its own item); dev fixtures reapplied do not remove Sessions or schedules created by hand. The design now draws what shipped: AW03 «Una sola vez» / «Cada semana» with «Día de la semana» and «Vigente desde» (generates 30 days ahead), «Solo esta sesión» / «Esta y las futuras» on a series Session, a one-off Session without the choice; AW07's required «Descripción»; AW17 cards with Borrador / Programada / Activa / Finalizada; AW18 «Guardar borrador» / «Publicar» (disabled until complete), «Guardar cambios» / «Dejar de publicar», and the image-change warning; purely informational sample Promotions on AW17, AW18, and 02; AW04 with the six catalogue plans. Decided under the blanket approval: no «Archivada» card (a deleted Promotion is archived and leaves the list, as built); «Vigente desde» may be today, as built. Gap from the follow-up 39 export: AW04's count line «6 planes · 6 visibles en la app» is not built (the page shows only its subtitle), and the development fixtures seed four of the six catalogue plans. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 53 is in AW02–AW07 and AW19–AW20 (cancel only a booked Session, read-only Class Type on edit, «Terminar la serie», Plan order and visibility, archive actions and Estado filters); the field label reads «Entrenadora» per 59 §6. |

Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium 1228 (playwright-core 1.61.0 from the scratchpad) against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (locale es-BO, zone Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (zone Asia/Tokyo): Clientes listed 6 Clients (one, «Ana Nueva», is an earlier session's leftover) with «Sin plan» for Mariana; «guzman» found Sofía Guzmán and put `?q=guzman` in the address; «rodrigues» showed the three-line no-results body under the header with «Mostrando 0 de 6 clientas»; Lucía's panel showed 13/09/2026 – 13/10/2026 and «5 de 8», Mariana's «Sin plan activo»; Personal showed 8 members with neutral Role tags and «Todas» for Paola; the Entrenador filter put `?role=trainer` in the address; empty submit marked Nombre, Correo, and Rol; Administrador swapped the chips for the read-only row; a test Assistant was created («Invitación enviada a …»), its immediate resend answered the too-soon message (`429`), its Role changed to Entrenador, and it was deactivated after the confirmation; removing Urubó from Emma Vargas was refused with the Horario explanation (`409`); Paola's own row opened read-only; Horario's Trainer list for Centro stayed Daniela Ortiz and Emma Vargas (the deactivated test member excluded); at 420 px cards, the pinned search, and full-screen panels. Fixed from the walk: the browser's blue search clear button (off-palette) is hidden, the Personal counter counts «miembros», and the read-only contact labels no longer wrap. No console errors besides the pre-login `401` probe and the expected `429` and `409`. The walk's two test Staff Members and their Accounts and tokens were deleted from `pilates_dev_sim`; their six append-only `staff_member.*` audit records stay, and their queued activation-email jobs (no worker ran) will find no Account. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: a new Administrator-only directory and detail instead of widening the Assistant search (bounded to 20, open to Assistants) or the overview (which carries Bookings and history AW22 does not show); search is the only filter, sent as `q`, 1–100 characters; the directory counts only Clients with an Account, like the Assistant search; `subscriptionStatus` is `none` for a Client who never bought a Plan, shown as a neutral «Sin plan» pill because «Expirado» would be false (follow-up 54 §1); the footer reads rows on the page over every Client, as follow-ups 25 and 28 draw it; `referredClientCount` counts every Referral made, pending or rewarded; a pending Account shows only its state, because the Activation Link belongs to System Identity and is managed from the Staff app; AW23's name search is not built (the backend has none and the Role filter covers a handful of people); the staff panel opens from local state like WEB-003's panels; name, email, and phone are read-only after creation because no command edits them; a Role change carries the Branches in the same command, a Branch-only change uses the Branch command, and an unchanged form sends nothing; the Branch warning is written to match the backend, which refuses rather than silently orphaning a Trainer's Sessions; «Restablecer contraseña» is not offered (ADR-0029 has no staff-assisted recovery; follow-up 54 §4); staff mutations invalidate only the `/administrator/staff-members` list prefix, which covers the Personal table and Horario's `branchTrainersQueryOptions`, never Branches or Sessions (asserted in `staff-create.spec.tsx` and `staff-edit.spec.tsx`); the Clientes page has no mutations. Reuse decisions: `CatalogPage` now serves read-only lists (optional create action, title from the row, counter wording, page notice) instead of a second list layout; `DataTable` draws its empty message inside the table body for every list; `ChoiceChips` gained a layout class and a `MultiChoiceChips` sibling; `branchTableQueryOptions` and `activeBranchesQueryOptions` are reused for Branch names and the picker; `formatBolivianos`, `formatCivilDate`, and `boliviaParts` for money and dates. Open questions for the user (data governance, not decided): may an Administrator see every Client's email, phone, Credit, and referral code in AW22, or should some be masked, and should reading a Client's detail be audited (reads are not audited today, ADR-0055)? How long are deactivated Staff Members, their Accounts, and invitations that expired kept (BE-007)? Gaps: pending-invitation email correction and cancellation (follow-up 35) and reactivation have no command yet; the staff resend throttle answers without `Retry-After`, so no retry time is shown (follow-up 38 asks for the server's); archived Branches still assigned to a member cannot be re-saved (`invalid_branch_assignment`); valid activation links still cannot be opened from the local email capture; the bundle grew to 699 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010.
