Curl on `pilates_dev_sim`: Paola 200 with `Cache-Control: no-store`, Camila (Assistant) 403, anonymous 401, `status=completed` 400, and the Staff app's `/payments/pending` still 200. Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium 1228 (playwright-core 1.61.0 from the scratchpad) against `node dist/src/main.js` and `node dist/src/worker-main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (locale es-BO, zone Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (zone Asia/Tokyo), with transfers raised through the Client API (upload, worker verification, Intent, receipt, submit) using a rendered sample bank receipt: Pagos showed Mariana Peña `Bs 560,00` and a throwaway Client `Bs 1.050,00` as Pendiente at `23/09/2026 · 22:51` in both zones, the badge «2», and Sofía's earlier Payment as «Bs 500,00 · con Bs 60,00 de crédito» Confirmado under Todos; the review panel loaded the receipt image; «Ver en grande» opened the viewer above the panel, and a typed rejection reason survived closing it with the scrim; rejecting Mariana showed the notice and the badge fell to 1; a second Mariana transfer rejected through the Staff app's bearer API while its panel was open made «Confirmar pago» answer `409` and the panel show «Otra persona ya decidió este pago.» with Rechazado; confirming the throwaway Client closed the panel, and Clientes, cached before as «Sin plan», showed «Mensual 16 clases · Activo»; at 420 px cards without horizontal scroll, the drawer badge, the full-screen panel, the full-screen receipt sheet with the ✕ top right, the confirmation dialog, the 301-character reason refused on its field, and «El comprobante ya no está disponible.» after marking that receipt deleted (restored afterwards). Fixed from the walk: a settled rejection now names its reason (`072b701`). A washed-out «Confirmar rechazo» in one screenshot was the button's color transition mid-animation; it read enabled 600 ms later and in the DOM. No console errors besides the pre-login `401` probes and the expected `409`. Left in `pilates_dev_sim` (payments, their Intents, Subscriptions, and the Credit ledger are append-only, and the ledger trigger refuses even a cascaded delete, so the cleanup transaction rolled back whole): Mariana Peña's two rejected transfers and one pending transfer (Bs 560, ready for Test 27), the throwaway Client «Camila Web Cinco» (`web005.camila@example.com`) with her confirmed transfer and Mensual 16 clases Subscription, their receipt images, notifications, and three append-only `payment.*` audit records. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: AW28 lists transfers only, because cash and Credit-only Payments are confirmed when recorded, cannot be rejected, and never wait for review (booking CONTEXT, Payment; ADR-0006; AW28 «every transfer Payment»; AW29 «solo llegan transferencias»); a new paged admin-web operation instead of widening the Staff app's pending-only array (ADR-0061/0062, ADR-0016); the backend's `status` defaults to `all` while the screen opens on Pendiente (AW28), and in Todos pending rows come first, oldest first, then decided ones newest decision first; `pendingCount` rides on every page so the subtitle and the sidebar badge need no second endpoint; the list refuses non-Administrators with `administration_access_denied` like the other admin-web oversight reads, while the Staff app's review endpoints keep `booking_access_denied` (the admin web only branches on 403); the receipt is shown from the existing authenticated, `no-store` `…/receipt` route through the same-origin proxy and the host-only session cookie rather than a new five-minute signed URL: ADR-0071's concern is a bearer URL that leaks, and this route is not a credential by itself (an ended session or a deleted receipt shows «No pudimos mostrar el comprobante.» and Reintentar re-reads the review, which the session guard turns into Login); the image never enters the query cache; «Confirmar pago» asks first because it starts the Subscription and cannot be undone, while «Rechazar» keeps AW29's inline reason with «Confirmar rechazo» as its explicit second step (follow-up 55 §4); a conflict re-reads the review and tells a Payment decided elsewhere from one refused because the Client already holds an active Plan by the review's state, since both answer `payment_confirmation_conflict`; confirming never extends an active Subscription (the backend refuses it, unchanged); a decision refreshes every Payment list and that review, plus the Clientes directory and every Client detail after a confirmation (her Plan starts, and a first Payment may award Credit to a referrer the admin web cannot name) or only her detail after a rejection (reserved Credit released), and a conflict refreshes like a confirmation (asserted in `payment-decision-cache.spec.ts`); the review has `staleTime: 0` so each opening is current; the open panel stays in local state like WEB-003/004. Reuse decisions: `CatalogPage` gained `canOpen` and `panelDescription` (and `DataTable` `canClickRow`) instead of a payments-only list layout; `ConfirmDialog` gained a primary tone; AW22's summary rows were extracted for AW29; `SearchFilter`, `ListFilter`, `StatusPill`, `PageNotice`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, `SidePanel`, `formatBolivianos`, `boliviaParts`/`formatCivilDate`, `initials`, and the form primitives are reused; the receipt viewer follows the Staff app's behavior (thumbnail, «Ampliar»/«Ver en grande», full screen, no actions in the viewer) but not its code, because the Staff app fetches bytes into a data URI for React Native while the browser can load the authenticated route directly. Open questions for the user (money and data governance, not decided): what a rejection reason may contain, given the Client reads it verbatim in her app and notification (for example no bank details or other Clients' data) and whether it needs a list of standard reasons; whether reading a receipt or the Payment list should be audited (ordinary reads are not, ADR-0055); whether a decided receipt should stay viewable in the admin web for its 7-day post-decision window (today only pending rows open, as AW28 draws); how long rejected Payments and their reasons are kept (BE-007). Gaps: the sidebar count refreshes on this browser's decisions and page loads, not live when the Staff app decides (no background polling exists); the receipt route has no signed-URL variant for a future CDN; narrow rows use the shared labelled-card reflow rather than AW28's compact «Plan · fecha» card; the bundle grew to 715 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010; valid activation links still cannot be opened from the local email capture. Open for the user: follow-up 55 (with 33–40 and 52–54 still pending export) is ready to send to the design session. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 55 is in AW28/AW29 (Credit line, empty and no-results frames, price and Credit rows, confirmation before «Confirmar pago», decided and receipt-unavailable states, «tú» in both notes). |

Design follow-up 61 `3a21997`. **Backend change (recorded, not a migration):** the uploads controller kept its own `Bearer`-only parser, so the admin web's `AdminSession` cookie credential got `401` from `POST /uploads` although every other admin route accepts it; red `4b898bd` → `ed1cae3` switches it to the shared `authenticateBearer` (uploads unit 51/51, uploads e2e 5/5 on `pilates_test_fixtures`, `pnpm check:api` unchanged). Verified 2026-09-24: admin web Jest 60 suites/393 tests in band (+8 suites, +46 tests), `eslint .`, `tsc -b`, Prettier, and `vite build` clean (`promotions-route` chunk 23.7 kB, index unchanged at 427 kB); Playwright 10/10; no leftover `pilates_test_*`. Surface walk in the desktop app's built-in Chromium against `node dist/src/main.js` and `worker-main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px and 420 px, with three sharp-generated images served from the dev server and handed to the file input and drop target from the page: the empty carousel; a 1600 × 900 JPG declared, put, finalized, and `ready` after four polls with the 16:9 preview and caption; Título, Descripción, and the window typed, «Publicar» → «Promoción «Nueva sala de Reformer en Urubó» publicada.» and an **Activa** card at position 1 with its thumbnail; an 800 × 450 WEBP refused under the target («La imagen debe medir al menos 1200 × 675 píxeles.», no request); a 1200 × 1600 PNG previewed with the top and bottom bands dimmed and «se pierden los bordes superior e inferior», saved as a **Borrador** at position 2 with the centre-cropped thumbnail; the second card dragged above the first (`PUT …/order` 200, badges renumbered); the published card edited («Posición 2 en el carrusel», «Imagen actual · 1600 × 900», «Se está mostrando ahora: hoy está dentro del rango.», «Guardar cambios» / «Dejar de publicar» / «Eliminar promoción»), its image replaced with the portrait one (warning box, footer to «Guardar borrador» / «Publicar»), saved and seen dropping to **Borrador**, published again to **Activa**, then «Dejar de publicar» → **Borrador**; the draft deleted through the dialog («Promoción «Clases de Barre los sábados» eliminada.»); at 420 px the stacked cards with the handle on the left and the wrapped headline, the full-screen sheet with the dates stacked, no sideways scroll; console only the pre-login `401` probes (plus the one `401` that led to the backend fix). Test 32C with curl while the first card was Activa: `GET /api/v1/promotions` as Lucía `200` with `private, no-cache`, one Promotion with three WebP sources (480 × 270, 960 × 540, and 1200 × 810), the public 1440 copy fetched without a credential with `public, max-age=31536000, immutable` and `nosniff`; anonymous `401` and Lucía `403` on the Administrator routes. Rows left in `pilates_dev_sim`: the walk's two Promotions are archived (8 archived in total, 0 live), their six image assets are `deleted` and every promotion variant swept after a worker restart (empty asset folders remain under `.data/uploads`), and UPL-002's three failed `promotion` uploads still wait for the 24-hour cleanup. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: AW17 is an unpaged card list, not a `CatalogPage` table, with no search and no Branch scope; the state pill tones are Borrador neutral, Programada Advertencia, Activa Éxito, Finalizada Error (like «Venció» in Mi plan); the order on screen is the saved one until a drop, a drop persists the whole list and the answer replaces the cache (no optimistic renumbering, so no equal positions ever show), a refused drop restores the saved order with a role=alert line; keyboard reordering is the accessible route the handle must offer; the thumbnail and the 1440 px preview are plain `<img>` elements on the private route with the session cookie, never in the query cache, with the asset id appended to the URL; «Publicar» disabled until every field exists (follow-up 39) while «Guardar borrador» always saves; a published Promotion's footer is «Guardar cambios» / «Dejar de publicar» and only an image change turns it into the draft pair; «Publicar» on a new Promotion creates then publishes, and if the publish step fails the panel keeps the created row so a retry publishes instead of creating twice; delete asks first and archives (BKM-001); the image is checked in the browser (format, weight, decoded size) before the three calls, and a refused file never leaves the browser; the worker's `failed` verdict shows a generic message because the status route names no reason; the same `Idempotency-Key` per typed body for create, a fresh UUID per finalize; the window line and the image-change warning use the typed dates and the saved row, not a re-read; no drag library: pointer events and the existing primitives suffice (about 200 lines, no dependency commit). Reuse decisions: the browser upload stays in the admin web (`api/upload-image.ts`) rather than a shared package: the Client app's `receipt-upload.ts` reads files, hashes, and picks images through Expo and ends by attaching a receipt, so only the four-call order (about 25 lines) is common, and the next user is the mobile profile photo (Client 22), which would be Expo again; extract a runtime-neutral protocol package at that third user if the order changes; `useIdempotencyKey`/`idempotencyHeaders`, `ConfirmDialog`, `SidePanel`, `PageHeader`, `PageNotice`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, `StatusPill`, `formatCivilDate`/`boliviaToday`/`addDays`, `civilDateField` (added beside `pastCivilDateField`), the form primitives, and `closeButtonClass` are reused; `optionalSearch` was added to `components/table/pagination.ts` for every list route. Backend proposals (not done, no migration): expose the worker's reason on `UploadStatusDto` (`failureReason`) so the admin web can say `image_dimensions_too_small` / `image_animated_not_allowed` instead of the generic line; keep the original filename on the image asset (or accept «Imagen actual» in AW18, follow-up 61 §3); the `promotion_1440` variant of a source narrower than 1440 px loses 16:9 (sharp's `withoutEnlargement` with `cover` made 1200 × 810 from a 1200 × 1600 file, while 480 and 960 were exact): size the box from the source width in `uploads/image-variants.ts`; UPL-002's review DTO `mediaType` gap stands. Open questions for the user (data governance and product, not decided): how long archived Promotions and their audit history are kept (BE-007, UPL-002's question stands); what a Promotion may show beyond the 80/280 characters; whether a Promotion whose window ended should be re-publishable without a new window (the API allows it); whether reordering should be audited (UPL-002 left it unaudited). Gaps: the mid-drag visual (tilt, dimming, insertion line) was verified in Playwright, not screenshotted in the walk; the worker's refusal reason is generic until the status route carries it; the file picker filters formats through `accept`, so only a dropped file reaches the format refusal; Firefox and WebKit journeys still need `playwright install`; the Client carousel (MOB-019) is still not built. **Design follow-up 39 exported 2026-09-30** (`76a6f95`, status `5623d3b`): applied on a temporary Claude Design canvas (no TurboTenant design system), read back and copied byte for byte, rendered over a scratch copy of `design/` and checked against checklist 30 before the copy; the canvas was deleted. The design now draws what shipped: AW03 «Una sola vez» / «Cada semana» with «Día de la semana» and «Vigente desde» (generates 30 days ahead), «Solo esta sesión» / «Esta y las futuras» on a series Session, a one-off Session without the choice; AW07's required «Descripción»; AW17 cards with Borrador / Programada / Activa / Finalizada; AW18 «Guardar borrador» / «Publicar» (disabled until complete), «Guardar cambios» / «Dejar de publicar», and the image-change warning; purely informational sample Promotions on AW17, AW18, and 02; AW04 with the six catalogue plans. Decided under the blanket approval: no «Archivada» card (a deleted Promotion is archived and leaves the list, as built); «Vigente desde» may be today, as built. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 61 is in AW17/AW18. |

Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium 1228 (playwright-core 1.61.0 from the scratchpad) against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (locale es-BO, zone Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (zone Asia/Tokyo), in Centro and Equipetrol: empty-submit validation on AW09; three Centro Supplies created («Alcohol en gel» at its threshold turned the sidebar count to 1); «toallas de papel» refused as taken on its field; a Withdrawal of 7 over a stock of 6 marked «Solo hay 6 unid. en stock.» with «Registrar» disabled, then 2 recorded («quedan 4 unid.»); with the panel open on 4 units a concurrent Withdrawal of 3 through the API (201), then «Registrar» answered `409` and the panel showed «Solo hay 1 unid. en stock.» and «(1 unid.)»; a Restock of 12 («ahora hay 13 unid.»); a threshold edit cleared the badge; «Cera para piso» archived after the confirmation, left Insumos, and appeared in AW31's filter as «(archivado)»; AW31 for September listed Toallas and the earlier BKO-002 Withdrawals with «Consumo del mes», filtered to Toallas, and August showed the empty state; Equipetrol started empty and its «Jabón líquido» showed only there with its own count; Nómina with the studio-wide note, a tomorrow date refused, Emma Vargas paid Bs 3.500,00 and corrected to Bs 3.600,00, filtered in the address; Gastos in Centro with «Limpieza profunda» added inline by Enter and chosen, Bs 450,50 recorded and corrected to Bs 480,00, filtered, Equipetrol empty; Categorías with the studio-wide note, «Limpieza profunda» and «Reparación» «En uso», «Temporal» added, «temporal» refused, «Temporal» deleted after the confirmation; at 420 px the drawer count, the bottom-sheet selector, card tables with no horizontal scroll, full-screen panels, a Withdrawal over the stock refused and one of 1 recorded, a Restock of 6, the history cards, and the inline row. Fixed from the walk: `6f69906`, `1493bcc`, `a9bc287`, `66a170d`. No console errors besides the pre-login `401` probes and the expected `409`s. Left in `pilates_dev_sim` (the schema refuses deleting Supplies, Salary Payments, and Expenses and updating or deleting movements): Centro's «Toallas de papel», «Alcohol en gel», and archived «Cera para piso», Equipetrol's «Jabón líquido», three Withdrawals (Toallas 2 and 3, Alcohol 1), two Restocks (Toallas 12, Alcohol 6), Emma Vargas's Bs 3.600,00 Salary Payment of 24/09/2026, the «Limpieza profunda» category and its Bs 480,00 Centro Expense, and their four append-only `salary_payment.*`/`expense.*` audit records; «Temporal» was deleted. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: **Categorías de gasto is studio-wide** (BKO-001 D13: one set of labels for every Branch), so AW15 has no Branch column, the selector shows its studio-wide note there, and follow-up 56 §1 asks the design system and AW34 to say so; the Supply list became page-and-offset with a status filter rather than client-side paging over the array (ADR-0061/0062, as WEB-003 did), and the low-stock count rides on every page so the sidebar needs no second endpoint; the sidebar count and both movement panels read the same one-page-of-100 active list, so a movement refreshes both at once; AW08 shows only active Supplies and AW31's filter every Supply including archived ones; a Withdrawal or Supply rename/archive refreshes that Branch's Supply lists and withdrawal history, a create or Restock only its Supply lists (a Restock adds no Withdrawal), never another Branch's (asserted in `back-office-cache.spec.ts`); a Salary Payment refreshes every payroll page and only the all-Branches expense report (payroll is studio-wide, BKO-001 Q3); an Expense refreshes its Branch's Expenses, that Branch's and the all-Branches report, and the category list (its «En uso»); a category change refreshes only the category list; the stock shown next to each Insumo lives in the option label, which is «next to the selected item» on every browser; the withdrawal quantity is checked against the stock last read and a server refusal re-reads it, the database keeping the last word (D4); the Idempotency-Key is kept per request body, so a retried lost answer replays and an edited body never reuses a key (`idempotency_key_reused`); Withdrawals and Restocks have no edit or delete anywhere (immutable, D6) and Salary Payments and Expenses only a correction (Q1); dates are no later than today in Bolivia on the client and the server (Q2); the Expense's Branch is shown as a line in its panel because AW14 has no field for it (D11); deleting an unused category asks first because it cannot be undone; the AW31 month picker is a stepper instead of a native month input, which Safari and Firefox do not draw; the history link carries `?page=1&pageSize=20` because TanStack Router's types require search for the nested route. Reuse decisions: `CatalogPage` gained panel actions and links instead of a second list layout for AW08; `DataTable`, `SidePanel`, `ConfirmDialog`, `ListFilter`, `StatusPill`, `PageNotice`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, the form primitives, `wholeNumberField`, `formatBolivianos`, `formatCivilDate`/`formatCivilMoment`, and the sidebar badge are reused; Horario's key predicate and no-Branch page were extracted at their second use; the Plan form's bolivianos field was extracted for the two Back Office amounts; one `CategoryAdder` serves AW14 and AW15; one problem mapper serves every Back Office code. Open questions for the user (money and data governance, not decided): may every Administrator see every Staff Member's pay in Nómina, or should payroll amounts be restricted or masked (payroll is personal data; BE-007)?; should corrections of a Salary Payment or Expense be readable somewhere (the audit records hold previous values, but no screen shows them)?; should a Withdrawal recorded by mistake get a void, since offsetting it with a Restock fixes the stock but leaves AW31's monthly consumption overstated (BKO-001 D6)? Gaps: Restocks have no read-back screen (AW31 lists Withdrawals only); native date pickers follow the browser's language (`mm/dd/yyyy` in an English Chrome), as in WEB-003; the Insumos count refreshes on this browser's changes and page loads, not live; the movement panels and AW31's filter read at most 100 Supplies per Branch; AW31 keeps a `supplyId` from another Branch after the selector changes (it shows the empty state); the bundle grew to 753 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010; Back Office still has no unit-level specs (its adapters are integration-tested and the new transport parser is covered by e2e); AW16 (WEB-009) still has to combine `reports/income` and `reports/expenses`. Open for the user: follow-up 56 (with 33–40 and 48–55 still pending export) is ready to send to the design session. **Design follow-ups 48–51 exported 2026-09-30** (`43fd815`): applied on a temporary Claude Design canvas, read back and copied byte for byte after a render check against checklist 30 (its §6 now counts four AW08 actions); canvas deleted. AW08 lists «+ Nuevo insumo», «Registrar retiro», «Registrar reposición», «Ver historial de retiros» and draws the restock panel («Cantidad repuesta», whole units from 1); AW09's edit frame ends with «Archivar insumo» and its «¿Archivar este insumo?» dialog (Cancelar / Archivar, as 49 wrote; 59 §8 changes it to Volver); AW10 shows «Máximo: N unid.» and the «Solo hay N unid. en stock.» error with «Registrar» disabled; AW16 labels the payroll row «Nómina» («Viene de Nómina, no de las categorías») and adds a single-Branch frame (Centro) without it, with «Gastos registrados en Centro», «Pagos en efectivo cobrados en Centro», and the caption above the figures (59 §7 over 51 §2, as built). The shipped admin web already matches. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 56 is in the design system, AW08–AW15, AW31, and AW34; samples were adapted to Sucursal Equipetrol, AW11/AW12 sample Roles became Entrenadora/Asistente/Administración, and AW11–AW14 regained the Gastos and Categorías de gasto links checklist 30 §5 requires. Fixed from the walk: `5c04e22`. Cleanup: the three Evaluations were cancelled (rows stay `cancelled`; Evaluations are never deleted), the Equipetrol block ended from 2026-09-25, and Gabriela is back to Centro only. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: **Evaluaciones nutricionales is Branch-scoped** (an Evaluation happens at a Branch, ADR-0001 and the glossary) while Referidos and Créditos stay studio-wide as `navigation.ts` marks them; AW25 reads a Bolivia week with Horario's stepper (the export already drew week arrows) rather than a free date range; the Nutricionista filter lists the Branch's currently assigned Nutritionists, deactivated included; the kind is only first/follow-up (the domain keeps no ordinal, so AW25's «Control · 3.ª evaluación» becomes «Seguimiento» as on S15); the narrow layout is one card per Evaluation like every other list instead of the export's day grouping; the Administrator Evaluation cancel (`DELETE /administrator/evaluations/{id}`) stays off the admin web because AW25 is read-only and invariant 10 allows no detail; Referral counts and the referrer total ignore the search, while the footer counts the matching rows; the Credit total block shows only without a search, because a searched page would not add up to it; «Última actividad» names all four ledger movements instead of the export's two; the URL keeps `q` like Clientes and Pagos and the query maps it to the backend's `query`. Reuse decisions: `CatalogPage` gained a panel-less mode and a footer slot instead of a second read-only layout; `WeekStepper` extracted at its second use; `staffOptionLabel` reused from Nómina; one `nameSearchSchema` serves AW26 and AW27; `DataTable`, `ListFilter`, `SearchFilter`, `StatusPill`, `NoBranchPage`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, `formatBolivianos`, and `formatCivilDate` reused. Open questions for the user (data governance, not decided): should every Administrator see every Client's Credit balance and every Evaluation's Client name (Evaluation data handling is BE-007)?; should an Administrator be able to cancel an Evaluation from the admin web at all, given the route exists and AW25 is read-only? Gaps: a Nutritionist who lost the Branch assignment no longer appears in its filter, although her past Evaluations there stay under «Todas»; AW25 lists booked Evaluations only (cancelled ones never show, as ADM-001 D1 decided); Referral search and Credit search keep the ADM-001 cap of 80 characters and the backend's `query` name; the Credit list pages its balances in memory after one grouped query (fine for a studio's size, not for thousands of Clients); AW16 (WEB-009) is next. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 57 is in AW25–AW27. |

Browser walk in Playwright-driven headless Chromium 1228 against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` and `pnpm dev:admin`, as Paola, at 1440 px (es-BO, Europe/Madrid) and 420 px (Asia/Tokyo), after recording a Bs 350,00 Reparación Expense at Equipetrol for 15/07/2026 through the API: Todas (01/04–24/09/2026) showed Ingresos Bs 5.690,00 (Efectivo Bs 4.140,00 · Transferencias Bs 1.550,00), Gastos Bs 8.380,50 with Nómina Bs 7.100,00, Neto −Bs 2.690,50, bars only in July, August, and September, and September's popover «Ingresos Bs 4.910,00 / Gastos Bs 8.030,50»; Centro showed only its cash Bs 4.140,00 and its Bs 930,50 of Expenses, no Nómina row, the caption, and `?branch=` in the address; Equipetrol showed −Bs 350,00, and from 01/09 Bs 0,00 in all three; a backwards range kept the address and showed the alert; confirming Mariana Peña's pending Bs 560,00 transfer in Pagos raised Todas to Bs 6.250,00 (transfers Bs 2.110,00) without reloading while Centro stayed at Bs 4.140,00; at 420 px the tiles stacked, the chart kept its pairs, both tables became cards, the top-bar note was gone, no horizontal scroll, no console errors besides the pre-login `401` probe. Fixed from the walk: `1073111`, `054a0d4`. Left in `pilates_dev_sim` (the schema refuses deleting Expenses and Payment decisions are final): the Equipetrol Expense of Bs 350,00 (with its `expense.recorded` audit row) and Mariana Peña's confirmed transfer (her Paquete de 8 clases started, with `payment.transfer_confirmed`). Decisions taken under the blanket approval: the report opens on «Todas las sucursales» (the one view the shell cannot give) and the last six Bolivia months up to today; its Branch selector lists archived Branches too, since their history still counts; Branch and range live in the address, and the shell's Branch choice neither drives nor follows it; the range is two native date fields (inclusive Bolivia dates) sent as Bolivia-midnight instants, `to` exclusive by a day; the report queries keep no placeholder data, so a scope switch shows the loading line instead of the previous scope's figures under the new label; amounts carry centavos everywhere (the export's «Bs 281.200» drops them; ADR-0082 and the design system write `Bs 1.050,00`); the net is signed «−Bs» in Plomo cálido; the Todas Ingresos tile splits cash and transfers (Credit-only Payments are Bs 0 by definition, so they are not shown); the payroll row is «Nómina» as follow-up 51 names it; the chart is HTML/CSS bars rather than a chart library (no dependency is justified for two series), and because Éxito and Error are close for deuteranopes (the dataviz validator: chroma floor FAIL for Éxito, deutan ΔE 6,6) it carries a legend, a 2 px gap between each pair, a per-month popover, and the month table; the closed palette is kept rather than changing the prescribed colours. Reuse decisions: `ListFilter`, `Input`, `PageHeader`, `QueryFailure`/`LoadingNotice`, the `Table` primitives with their card reflow, `formatBolivianos`, `formatCivilDate`, `formatMonth`, `addMonths`, and `boliviaInstant` reused; the report's route lives in `app/report-routes.tsx` beside WEB-008's route files. Open questions for the user (money and data governance, not decided): should income count Payments by the day they were confirmed (today, as ADM-001 decided) or by the day the transfer was sent?; should the report subtract Credit applied to Payments or show it as a separate line (income today is the amount the Client paid, net of Credit)?; may every Administrator see total payroll in «Todas las sucursales» (BKO-001 Q3, BE-007)? Gaps: the report has no export (CSV/PDF) and no comparison with a previous period; a very long range draws one narrow pair per month (no yearly grouping); native date pickers and the select arrow follow the browser (the select's arrow is still the browser's); the bundle grew to 779 kB, so route-level splitting is due before WEB-010. **Design follow-ups 48–51 exported 2026-09-30** (`43fd815`): applied on a temporary Claude Design canvas, read back and copied byte for byte after a render check against checklist 30 (its §6 now counts four AW08 actions); canvas deleted. AW08 lists «+ Nuevo insumo», «Registrar retiro», «Registrar reposición», «Ver historial de retiros» and draws the restock panel («Cantidad repuesta», whole units from 1); AW09's edit frame ends with «Archivar insumo» and its «¿Archivar este insumo?» dialog (Cancelar / Archivar, as 49 wrote; 59 §8 changes it to Volver); AW10 shows «Máximo: N unid.» and the «Solo hay N unid. en stock.» error with «Registrar» disabled; AW16 labels the payroll row «Nómina» («Viene de Nómina, no de las categorías») and adds a single-Branch frame (Centro) without it, with «Gastos registrados en Centro», «Pagos en efectivo cobrados en Centro», and the caption above the figures (59 §7 over 51 §2, as built). The shipped admin web already matches. **Design follow-ups 53–62 exported 2026-09-30** (`c2990a2`, one canvas, byte-for-byte copy, canvas deleted): follow-up 58 is in AW16; its figures use the new «Cifra» step (Lora 500 · 32/1.2 · tabular) that 59 §10 adds. |
| WEB-010 | P2       | Admin quality          | Add Jest/Testing Library/MSW coverage, Playwright critical journeys, Vercel preview/proxy verification, and production deployment               | WEB-001–WEB-009, EXT-003, WEB-006 | AW01–AW34                  | blocked | Route-level code splitting (refactor only, ADR-0023) `0cf7827`: every page route's `component` is TanStack Router's `lazyRouteComponent`, so the router preloads a page's chunk on hover intent and loads it on first visit; each page gained a `*-route.tsx` beside it (`schedule-route.tsx`, `branches-route.tsx`, …) that reads its typed search and navigation through `getRouteApi("/authenticated/…")` instead of importing the route object, and `router.tsx`/`back-office-routes.tsx`/`oversight-routes.tsx`/`report-routes.tsx` keep only paths, search schemas, and the lazy components; the entry keeps the session guard, the shell, the sign-in pages, the placeholder page, and the search schemas. `vite build` before: one `index-*.js` of 778.96 kB (gzip 225.34 kB) plus 32.83 kB CSS, over Vite's 500 kB hint; after: `index-*.js` 426.5 kB (gzip 138.1 kB), a shared `button-*.js` (Radix, shadcn, forms) 177.4 kB, `data-table-*.js` 48.2 kB, `schedule-route-*.js` 27.1 kB, the other pages 2.9–13.9 kB each (Pagos 13.9, Personal 13.3, Reporte 11.6, Insumos 9.1), 33.15 kB CSS, no chunk over 500 kB and no warning; specs stayed green with no behaviour change. Playwright (ADR-0043): dependency `64ee791` (`@playwright/test` 1.61.0, whose Chromium is the cached build 1228; the `playwright` package has no build script, so `pnpm-workspace.yaml` is unchanged), config `32f5246` (`playwright.config.ts`, `e2e/support/{environment,global-setup,session}.ts`, `tsconfig.e2e.json` as a third project reference, an ESLint override with Node globals for `e2e/**`, `test:e2e` and the Prettier globs in the app, root `pnpm test:admin-e2e` that builds the backend and the admin web first, `test-results`/`playwright-report` ignored), journeys `bc19ccb` (Login to Horario with the host-only HttpOnly Secure Lax cookie unreadable from scripts, logout from the menu, a signed-out visit returning to `/catalogo/planes?page=2` after Login, a session ended behind the browser's back sending the next click to Login with «Tu sesión terminó» and back to Nómina afterwards), `2f5fd5a` (the Branch selector scoping Horario: Centro's Mat Pilates against Equipetrol's Pilates Prenatal on next week, the week staying in the address), `84d299c` (Personal's `role=trainer` in the address, surviving a reload), `c21f085` (an Expense of Bs 450,50 recorded at Centro with «Mantenimiento» typed into the inline category row, then Equipetrol still empty), `02810d8` (Camila the Assistant refused neutrally; a cookie request from `https://attacker.example` refused `403 untrusted_origin` while the session stays valid), `a5bafe8` (420 px: drawer, bottom-sheet selector, one card per member, no sideways scroll). The suite's global setup creates `pilates_test_<hex>` on the `pilates_test_fixtures` admin database through `psql`, runs `prisma migrate deploy` and `development-fixtures-main.js` with `DATABASE_URL` set explicitly (the backend's `.env` is never read for the database), starts `node dist/src/main.js` on 3101 with `ADMIN_WEB_TRUSTED_ORIGINS=http://localhost:4173` and `vite preview` on 4173 (whose proxy inherits `server.proxy`), waits for `/health/ready`, and returns a teardown that stops both by pid and drops the database; nine journeys in about 15 s, Chromium only, one worker, locale es-BO in a Europe/Madrid clock. Verified 2026-09-24: backend unit 99/100 suites, 456/457 tests (only the known `pg-boss-runtime.spec`); e2e 27/27 suites, 147/147 tests with `DATABASE_URL` on `pilates_test_fixtures`; integration 53/53 suites, 216/216 tests via `TEST_DATABASE_URL`, no leftover `pilates_test_*` databases; `pnpm check:api` clean, `check:api-compat` 2/2; admin web Jest 49 suites/330 tests (+1 suite, +7 tests) green twice in parallel and once in band, after `e519f95` raised Testing Library's lookup timeout to three seconds (a cold parallel worker's first whole-app render, in the two slowest suites Jest schedules first, kept outlasting the one-second default; assertions are unchanged); `eslint .`, `tsc -b` (app, specs, and e2e), Prettier (src, e2e, config), and `vite build` clean; Playwright 9/9 through `pnpm test:admin-e2e`, its database dropped and ports 3101/4173 free afterwards. Browser walk in the desktop app's built-in Chromium against `node dist/src/main.js` on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded) with `vite preview` on 4173 (the split build) and `pnpm dev:admin` on 5173, as Paola, at 1440 px and 420 px: every route (Horario, the three catalog pages, the eight Back Office pages with Retiros, Reporte, Promociones, Referidos, Créditos, and `/no-existe`) loaded its own chunk on the preview build with no console error besides the pre-login `401` probe; the Visibilidad, Categoría, and Insumo selects and the Nuevo gasto panel's select drew the tenue chevron; choosing «Toallas de papel» in Retiros and switching to Equipetrol emptied the Insumo filter and the address (`?month=2026-09&page=1&pageSize=20`); at 420 px no page scrolled sideways, the drawer, the bottom-sheet selector, the card rows, the stacked report tiles, and the day tabs behaved as before; Gastos on the dev server at 420 px loaded with an empty console. The walk created nothing (reads and selector changes only), so nothing was deleted; earlier walks' rows (Toallas de papel, Cera para piso, Emma's two Salary Payments, the Equipetrol Expense) are still visible. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: lazy components sit on the route's `component` (not inside a wrapper) so intent preload fetches the chunk before the click, and the wrapper modules use `getRouteApi` by id rather than importing the route object, which would be a circular import; no `pendingComponent` was added, so the main area is blank for the milliseconds a cold chunk takes (the sidebar hover preload covers the usual case), recorded as a gap; the sign-in pages stay in the entry because they are the first thing a visitor needs; `preloadAdminPages` is exported from `router.tsx` because it is also what a browser would call to warm the pages after Login; a foreign filter is forgotten by an effect once the options are known (and page 1 restored), never by rendering a select whose value has no option; the select keeps its native element (ADR-0040 prefers semantic HTML) and only its arrow changes; Playwright journeys start each from a fresh sign-in and share one database in file order, so the Expense journey runs on the empty Gastos the fixtures leave; the session-expiry journey revokes the session through `page.request` (the browser's own cookie jar) because the 30-minute idle expiry cannot be waited for, and it ends in the same `401 authentication_required` path; the authorization journey is the Assistant's neutral refusal plus the `untrusted_origin` refusal, because the fixtures hold one Administrator, a second one cannot be activated without the email capture, and Paola cannot demote herself, so the page's 403 state stays component-tested (every list spec covers it); Playwright's `webServer` was not used because it starts before `globalSetup` and the database name is only known there; the teardown is the function `globalSetup` returns; the e2e backend runs on 3101 so a development backend on 3100 is never disturbed; only Chromium is configured because it is the one browser cached (the config names the `playwright install` step for the others). Reuse decisions: `useKnownOption` was extracted at its second use (AW31 and AW25) into `components/table` beside the other filter helpers; the chevron reuses the date icon's approach in `index.css` rather than a Radix Select; the e2e sign-in helper and fixture accounts live in `e2e/support` and are not shared with the Jest fixtures (different runtimes). Open questions for the user (money and data governance, not decided): none new; WEB-004, WEB-005, WEB-007, WEB-008, and WEB-009's questions stand. Gaps, still open in this row: the Vercel preview, the real proxy (forwarded address, `trust proxy`, preview-origin registration), and the production deployment need EXT-003's accounts; Promociones landed in WEB-006 (its Playwright journey makes the suite ten); CI does not run any of these suites (EXT-003); Firefox and WebKit journeys need `playwright install`; a run killed before its teardown leaves its backend, preview, and database behind (Test 31 says how to find them); the blank main area while a cold chunk loads has no pending line; native date pickers still follow the browser's language; gaps that need backend work stay where their rows left them (Restock read-back, cancelled Sessions in Horario, invitation email correction, «Log out all other devices»). |

Findings closed red → green: Horario Branch switch red `993fb9f`, spec correction `c1a5675` → `bdf5ff8` (`schedule-page.tsx` is now `SchedulePage` → `BranchSchedule key={selectedBranch.id}` like every other Branch-scoped page, so the week's `keepPreviousData` placeholder never survives a Branch change and the switch starts from «Cargando el horario…»; week stepping inside one Branch still keeps the previous week on screen); archived Branch re-save red `865a267` → `d3530e9` (`staff-edit-form.tsx` reads the active Branches the chips draw and compares and sends only the ids among them, so a member still assigned to a Branch archived since saves without `invalid_branch_assignment`, the stale assignment dropped rather than refused); restored-session expiry red `0dac122` → `aa15db3` (`authenticatedRoute.beforeLoad` calls `sessionExpiry.reset()` once the probe finds a principal, so a logout's `silence()` ends when another tab's session is restored by Back); search keystrokes red `b7ee39b` → `f72f9fb`, lint fix `60fa00d` (`SearchFilter` remembers the text it last sent in state; when the address catches up with that value the field keeps what was typed meanwhile and sends it next, while a value from elsewhere — Back, a link — still replaces the text; new `search-filter.spec.tsx`); foreign filters red `9224d9c` → `9066c81` (`useKnownOption` on Gastos' `expenseCategoryId`, Nómina's `staffMemberId`, and Reporte's `branch`, which falls back to «Todas las sucursales» instead of a report headed «Sucursal» with no name). Design deviations in code, red `50ad04c`/`bfd585c`/`60b3979` → `213d54c` (the `<main>` is `bg-superficie` per design/README.md's main area; the shell wrapper stays Arena behind it), `53c4315` (`closeButtonClass` in `sheet.tsx`: the 30 px bordered square on Superficie with the Palo de rosa hover border, shared by every Sheet close and the receipt viewer, which drew it alone before), `20f9eba` (`rounded-md` → `rounded-control` on the Role tag and referral-code chip, `rounded-sm` → `rounded-full` on the chart legend and popover swatches (10 px dots, as the status pill dots), `text-[17px]` → `text-cuerpo-grande` on the week and month steppers, `text-[14px]` → `text-celda` on the menu name, `text-[12px]` and `text-[11.5px]` → `text-ayuda` on the viewer caption, avatar initials, and status pills; `account-menu.tsx` builds its rows through `cn()`; new `design-tokens.spec.ts` scans `src` for `rounded-sm/md/lg/xl`, the four bracket sizes, and template-literal `className`s so they cannot return). Kept as design-specified sizes with no token, not touched: the 10 px/0.18em AW34 group label, the 12.5 px README subtitle, the 15 px Lora day numerals, the 11 px avatar initials in Pagos, and the 32 px report figure (follow-up 59 §10). Accessibility, red `5e882e9`/`5c7bf1e`/`04852fb`/`0e2d25f` → `80a69c4` (`useDocumentTitle`: «Gastos · Emma» per page from `navigationItemOf`, which maps AW31 to Insumos, «Iniciar sesión · Emma» and the other two auth pages, «Emma · Administración» for an unknown path; `<main id="contenido" tabIndex={-1}>` receives focus when the pathname changes (not on first load, not on a search change), behind a «Saltar al contenido» link that is the first Tab stop), `ac2d89c` (the Branch listbox keeps its roles with a roving tabindex: the selected option is the Tab stop and the one Radix focuses on open, ArrowUp/Down wrap, Home/End, Enter/Space choose; the narrow sheet's «Sucursal» label is a `SheetTitle`, so the dialog has a real heading), `bbf13aa` (after «Cerrar sesión» focus lands on «Cancelar», the least destructive control, and returns to the row on cancel), `19c8c57` (after «Rechazar» focus lands on «Motivo del rechazo» and returns to «Rechazar» on cancel), `a03bc68` (`FormItem` tracks whether a `FormDescription` renders through a layout effect; `FormControl` lists only the ids on screen, so a field without helper text has no `aria-describedby` until it has an error). Playwright harness: config `5bfd130`, `b117bd7` (Jest also matches `e2e/support/*.test.ts`, Playwright only `*.spec.ts`, the test project includes the Node spec and the e2e project excludes it, ESLint gives it Jest globals), red `bcbd722`, spec correction `5718c07` → `7a143ca` (`e2e/support/child-process.ts`: `isRunning` treats `signalCode` as ended, `stopChild` resolves on exit or after the grace period, killing a child that ignored SIGTERM, so a teardown can never hang; `serverStdio` writes each server's stderr to `test-results/e2e-backend.stderr.log` / `e2e-preview.stderr.log`, or inherits under `E2E_DEBUG`; the setup's first line says so). Hygiene: `cc98bbf` (`ScheduleField` loses `effectiveFrom`, which no refusal set, and `occurrence-form.tsx` its unreachable branch), `b319b90` (`PopoverAnchor`, `SheetClose`, `BoliviaParts`, and `ReadOnlyRow` no longer exported), `456a581` (`everyBranchesQueryOptions` in `catalog/branches/branch-queries.ts` (status `all`, one page of 100, items) shared by Personal, the read-only staff panel, and the report's selector, whose archived-last order and Retiros' Supply order share `archivedLast`, and whose Branch names share `namesById`, in `lib/named.ts`; the two Personal readers and the report now hit one cache key). Browser walk in the desktop app's built-in Chromium against `node dist/src/main.js` (pid 45635, port 3100, `ADMIN_WEB_TRUSTED_ORIGINS` covering 5173 and 4173) on `pilates_dev_sim` (fixtures reloaded), `vite` on 5173 (pid 45636) and `vite preview` on 4173 (pid 45637), as Paola, at 1440 px and 420 px: the tab read «Iniciar sesión · Emma», then «Horario · Emma», «Gastos · Emma», «Reporte · Emma», «Personal · Emma», «Pagos · Emma», «Insumos · Emma»; one «Emma» in the DOM at 1440 px (the sidebar cell) and one at 420 px (the top bar beside the hamburger); `<main>` computed `rgb(250, 247, 242)` (Superficie) on both servers; the Branch popover opened with Centro focused and tabindex 0/−1/−1, ArrowDown moved focus to Equipetrol, Enter chose it, and Horario showed «Sucursal Equipetrol · sala única» over «Cargando el horario…» before Equipetrol's four Sessions (the read took a few seconds under the load), never Centro's grid; after a reload the first Tab landed on the visible «Saltar al contenido» pill top left, Enter focused `#contenido`, and clicking Gastos in the sidebar left focus on `<main>`; `/back-office/gastos?expenseCategoryId=<unknown>&page=2` settled on `?page=1&pageSize=20` with Categoría «Todas», and `/reporte?branch=<unknown>` on `branch=all` with «01/08/2026 – 24/09/2026 · Todas las sucursales»; a throwaway Branch «Prueba WEB-011» created, assigned to Andrea Suárez, and archived through curl showed as «Equipetrol, Prueba WEB-011» in Personal, her panel drew Centro/Equipetrol/Urubó chips with only Equipetrol checked and a 30 × 30 px close button with a 1 px Borde on Superficie, ticking Urubó and Guardar sent `PUT …/branches` (200) and the row read «Equipetrol, Urubó» with «Guardamos los cambios de Andrea Suárez.»; «Cerrar sesión» in the account menu put focus on «Cancelar», Enter returned it to the «Cerrar sesión» row; at 420 px Personal drew one card per member with no sideways scroll, the selector opened as a bottom sheet headed «Sucursal» (an `h2`) with Centro focused, and the drawer listed every section; the preview build on 4173 opened Insumos signed in with the same title, wordmark, and main tone; no console errors besides the pre-login `401` probes (the dev server's duplicated then aborted reads are StrictMode's). No pending transfer exists in `pilates_dev_sim`, so the «Rechazar» focus stays covered by its spec only. Cleanup: Andrea's Branches restored to Equipetrol through curl, the «Prueba WEB-011» Branch deleted from `booking.branches` (its two `staff_member.branches_changed` audit records stay, ids only), all three servers stopped by pid and the ports confirmed free. Decisions taken under the blanket approval: Horario is remounted per Branch (the keying fix) rather than dropping `keepPreviousData`, so stepping weeks inside one Branch still keeps the previous week on screen; a stale archived Branch is dropped silently on save instead of refused with a message, because the chips cannot show it and the backend would refuse the whole save; the guard resets the expiry on every authenticated `beforeLoad` (a no-op while a session lives); the search field trusts the address only when it differs from what it last sent; the report's unknown Branch falls back to «all» (its only neutral value) while list filters fall back to none; the wordmark moves to the top bar only when the sidebar is hidden (narrow), because design/README.md draws it in the 236 px cell and AW34 frame 4 gives the narrow top bar no cell; `rounded-sm` swatches became dots (`rounded-full`), since the scale's smallest corner (8 px) would round a 10 px square anyway; the 11.5 px pill and 12 px caption and initials took `text-ayuda` (the closest token, 11.5 px) while the 10 px label, 12.5 px subtitle, 15 px numerals, 11 px initials, and 32 px figure stay bracketed as design-specified sizes (the guard spec names only the four sizes with a token); focus after «Cerrar sesión» goes to «Cancelar» (the least destructive control, as Radix's AlertDialog does) and after «Rechazar» to the reason (the next step), returning to the row or button on cancel; the Branch selector keeps its listbox roles with a roving tabindex instead of becoming plain buttons, so the e2e journeys and specs keep `option`/`aria-selected`; `FormControl` learns of a description through a layout effect on `FormItem`'s context, at the cost of one extra render per field with helper text; the harness spec lives in `e2e/support/*.test.ts` under Jest (Playwright matches `*.spec.ts` only) because the helper is Node code the app's `src` project cannot type-check; `stopChild` never waits past the grace period, even if the child ignores SIGKILL's exit event; stderr goes to files by default and to the terminal under `E2E_DEBUG` so a normal run stays quiet. Reuse decisions: `everyBranchesQueryOptions` replaced two copies of the same read in Personal and a third with a different key in Reporte, so the three now share one cache entry; `namesById` and `archivedLast` extracted at their second use into `lib/named.ts`; `closeButtonClass` extracted from the receipt viewer at its second use; `useKnownOption` reused unchanged for three more filters; `useDocumentTitle` is one hook for the shell and the auth pages. ## Updating this backlog

Every edit to this file reaches `main` through a pull request like any other change (coding standards, «Branches and pull requests»). When starting work, change one item to `in progress`. When completing it, change
it to `done` and replace Evidence with the commit hash. If implementation exposes
a missing product rule, mark the item `blocked`, record the exact question in the
row or owning context, and resolve it before continuing that behavior.
